Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.

</details>


### [170] [Which one is banana man? Evaluating vision-language models in multi-turn pragmatic interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29571)
*Alvin Wei Ming Tan,Ben Prystawski,Veronica Boyce*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代指称游戏（iterated reference games）测试了人类与视觉-语言模型在语境敏感语用推理方面的能力，发现虽然模型能利用先前语境理解人类指称表达，但无法有效构建所需语境，缺乏高效语言协作的核心技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对话的流畅性依赖于对上下文的灵活适应和共享的语用直觉。迭代指称游戏为测试智能体在多轮语言环境中进行上下文敏感语用推理的能力提供了一个理想场景。研究者希望评估当前的视觉-语言模型是否具备类似人类的语用推理能力。

Method: 论文在迭代指称游戏中比较了人类参与者和视觉-语言模型的表现。实验系统地变化所提供的语境条件，包括语境的量、顺序和相关性三个维度，要求参与者识别迭代游戏中产生的描述性语言的目标含义。

Result: 人类参与者在所有语境条件下均表现稳定且良好。被评估的视觉-语言模型能够利用先前语境来解读人类的指称表达，但在有效构建解读这些表达所需的语境方面存在显著困难，整体表现远不如人类。

Conclusion: 当前视觉-语言模型虽然具备一定的上下文利用能力，但缺乏人类所拥有的构建与更新共享语境的核心技能，这使其难以在类似对话的协作场景中实现高效的语言合作，提示了现有模型在语用推理方面的根本性局限。

Abstract: Flexible adaptation to context and shared pragmatic intuitions contribute to smooth human conversation. Iterated reference games---in which players repeatedly pick out novel referents using language---present a test case for agents' ability to perform context-sensitive pragmatic reasoning in multi-turn linguistic environments. We tested humans and vision--language models on their ability to identify the intended meaning of descriptions produced in iterated reference games, varying the provided context in terms of amount, order, and relevance. While humans performed well consistently, the models we evaluated could make use of prior context to interpret humans' referring expressions, but they struggled to build up the relevant context to interpret those expressions effectively. Our results suggest that the models we evaluated lack core skills needed for efficient linguistic collaboration.

</details>


### [171] [SemTrace: Source-Grounded Semantic Signatures for Tracing LLM Exposure to Protected Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29575)
*Junyan Zhang,Yudong Zeng,Yongwei Huang,Zuhao Ouyang,Hong Chen,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SemTrace是一种基于源文档语义的水印方法，通过在受保护稿件中嵌入文档特定的二进制签名（从事实命题中构造），并利用指令跟随的审阅者在不改变独立评估的前提下表达这些事实，最终通过冻结的自然语言推理模型解码语义证据，实现模型无关的指定副本泄露检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用于读取文档并生成下游文本，这带来了溯源问题——当文档所有者无法控制或检查执行生成的模型时，无法追踪其受保护稿件是否被模型参考或影响。现有水印方法（如偏向token概率或施加表面形式模式）存在局限，需要一种与源文档语义绑定、不依赖特定模型的水印方案。

Method: SemTrace的核心方法包括：(1) 从手稿本身直接支持的客观事实命题中构建文档特定的二进制签名；(2) 受保护的PDF不可见地携带"内容契约"，从每对二元事实中选择一个事实，并要求指令跟随的审阅者在固定审阅槽中表达这些事实，而不改变其独立评估；(3) 使用冻结的自然语言推理模型解码语义证据，处理显式擦除并针对该副本分配的码字对恢复的比特进行评分。

Result: 该设计旨在实现模型无关的、针对指定副本的泄露检测（assigned-copy exposure detection），并保持水印与源文档语义紧密绑定。论文中应包含具体的实验验证结果以证明其有效性。

Conclusion: SemTrace提出了一种新颖的源端语义水印范式，与传统的概率偏向或表面形式水印不同，它将水印语义锚定于源文档的事实内容，通过内容契约和NLI解码实现可验证的副本追踪，为解决LLM文档溯源问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to read documents and produce downstream text, creating a provenance problem when the document owner cannot control or inspect the model that performs the generation. We introduce SemTrace, a source-grounded semantic watermark for detecting whether a generated review was influenced by a known protected manuscript copy. Rather than biasing token probabilities or imposing surface-form patterns, SemTrace constructs a document-specific binary signature from factual propositions that are directly supported by the manuscript itself. A protected PDF invisibly carries a content contract that selects one fact from each binary pair and asks an instruction-following reviewer to express those facts in fixed review slots without changing its independent evaluation. A frozen natural language inference model then decodes the resulting semantic evidence with explicit erasures and scores the recovered bits against the codeword assigned to that copy. This design targets model-agnostic, assigned-copy exposure detection while keeping the watermark semantically tied to the source document.

</details>


### [172] [SUP-MIMIC: A Multi-Task Clinical Diagnosis Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs' Robustness to Contradictory Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29582)
*Yi Yu,Bo Wang,Chong Feng,Ge Shi,Xia Liu,Ziyi Yang,Xuewen Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SUP-MIMIC框架，基于MIMIC-IV-v3.1构建多任务评估体系（BA、DDT、DCT），专门测试LLM在临床诊断中的"一对多"歧义消解和"多对一"诊断收敛能力，发现现有LLM在这些任务上相较于基线任务性能显著下降，暴露其依赖统计捷径而非因果推理的系统性问题，并揭示模型存在偏向"健康"预测的保守偏见，可能导致真实医疗场景中的漏诊风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估主要关注事实性知识检索，忽视了临床诊断中最根本的挑战——处理临床指标与诊断之间复杂的非一一映射关系。现有基准无法评估模型在诊断歧义场景（相同临床表现对应不同病因）和诊断收敛场景（异质症状指向同一疾病）中的推理能力，这限制了LLM在临床医学中的安全应用。

Method: 构建SUP-MIMIC多任务评估框架，包含三个任务：基础评估（BA）作为基线，诊断发散任务（DDT）评估"一对多"歧义消解能力（表型相似病例间的区分），诊断收敛任务（DCT）评估"多对一"诊断模式识别能力（跨不同病理生理通路的诊断归类），并对多款主流LLM进行综合评估。

Result: 综合评估显示LLM在DDT和DCT上相比基线任务（BA）出现显著的性能下降，揭示模型系统性地依赖统计捷径而非真正的因果推理。同时发现模型存在偏向"健康"预测的保守偏差，这在实际医疗场景中意味着非平凡的漏诊风险。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量化临床推理鲁棒性的严格方法论，并为提升语言模型在临床医学中的安全性提供了路线图。

Abstract: Current evaluations of large language models (LLMs) primarily focus on factual knowledge retrieval, overlooking the fundamental challenge of navigating the complex, non-bijective mappings between clinical indicators and diagnoses. Existing benchmarks fail to assess whether large language models truly possess the reasoning capability required for diagnostic ambiguity scenarios, where identical clinical presentations may correspond to different etiologies, and diagnostic convergence scenarios, where heterogeneous symptoms ultimately indicate the same disease. To address this issue, we propose SUP-MIMIC, a multi-task framework utilizing MIMIC-IV-v3.1 that comprises Basic Assessment (BA), Diagnostic Divergence Task (DDT), and Diagnostic Convergence Task (DCT). Specifically, DDT is designed to evaluate the model's "one-to-many" disambiguation capability among phenotypically similar cases, while DCT assesses the model's ability to identify "many-to-one" diagnostic patterns across different pathophysiological pathways. Comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs reveals substantial performance degradation on DDT and DCT compared to baseline tasks, exposing a systemic reliance on statistical shortcuts over genuine causal reasoning. Our findings further highlight a conservative bias toward "healthy" predictions, implying non-trivial risks for missed diagnoses in realistic medical settings. This work establishes a rigorous methodology for quantifying clinical reasoning robustness and provides a roadmap for enhancing the safety of language models in clinical medicine.

</details>


### [173] [How You Ask Shapes What You Get: A Theory-Seeded Measurement of Articulation in Advice-Seeking LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29591)
*Juneha Baek,Suhyeon Lee,Donghyuk Shin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将用户提问方式的"表述特征"(articulation)视为输入分布中稳定、可测量的结构，而非需被平均掉的噪声。作者从16,447条建议寻求类提示中提取出可复现的潜在表述因子，发现其与主题维度基本可分离，并识别出一种"长而信息贫乏"的表述风格（约占最大语料库1/6），该风格下模型回答更短更模糊且不主动追问澄清。文章主张基准测试应基于表述特征进行分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户以不同方式表达同样的建议寻求需求，先前工作将这种变化视为噪声而平均处理。作者认为这种变化是输入分布中稳定、可测量的结构，问题在于：(1)表述(how people ask)是否构成与主题(what they ask about)可分离的潜在维度；(2)表述是否与语言模型的响应方式相关联。由于这种结构对主题无关，传统的基于主题或任务的评估无法捕捉它。

Method: 从三个公共聊天语料库(WildChat、LMSYS、ShareChat)收集16,447条建议寻求类提示，提取可解释的特征，通过因子分析恢复少量潜在表述因子，并在训练/测试划分及不同语料库间验证其可复制性。使用人类标注者进行独立复现验证。

Result: 发现少量可复现的潜在表述因子，与主题维度基本可分离。识别出一系列反复出现的表述风格，其中一种"长形式但信息贫乏"的风格在最大语料库中约占1/6的提示，模型对此返回更短、更模糊的回答且不请求澄清——尽管欠规定恰是需要澄清的条件。该对比在每个主题组和长度五分位内均成立，且不是单纯欠规定所致（另一种同样欠规定的风格确实会引发澄清问题）。两位独立人类标注者复现了该对比。

Conclusion: 基准测试应当基于表述特征(stratify on articulation)进行分层评估，作者提供所提取的潜在结构作为实现此目的的测量工具。这项发现表明模型表现评估需要超越主题维度，纳入用户表述方式的系统性差异。

Abstract: Users articulate the same advice-seeking request in different ways: some specify detailed constraints, others gesture at a vague need. Prior work treats this variation as noise to be averaged away; we instead treat it as a stable, measurable structure in the input distribution. We ask whether articulation (how people ask) forms latent dimensions separable from topic (what they ask about), and whether it is associated with how language models respond. We extract interpretable features from 16,447 advice-seeking prompts pooled from public chat corpora (WildChat, LMSYS, and ShareChat) and recover a small set of latent articulation factors that replicate across train/test splits and across corpora. Because this structure is largely separable from topic, the populations it defines cut across topics and stay invisible to topic- or task-based evaluation. The factors define a handful of recurring articulation styles, one of which stands out: a long-form but information-poor style, roughly one in six prompts in the largest corpus, where models return shorter, vaguer answers and do not ask for clarification even though under-specification is exactly the condition that warrants it. The contrast holds within every topic group and length quintile, and is not under-specification alone -- a second, equally under-specified style does draw clarifying questions. Two independent human annotators reproduce this contrast. We argue that benchmarks should stratify on articulation, and we offer the extracted structure as a measurement instrument for doing so.

</details>


### [174] [Hindsight Memory-PRM: Supervising Memory Management with Auditable Hindsight Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29605)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Yingguang Yang,Yancheng Chen,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Qian Li,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Shang Luo,Kefu Xu,Hao Peng,Junyu Lu,Du Cheng,Philip S. Yu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Hindsight Memory-PRM，利用检索命中和引用等机器可读证据，为长视野LLM智能体的记忆操作提供无需人工标注的干预校准奖励信号，显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野LLM智能体的记忆操作难以监督——操作执行时的价值无法直接观测，且传统方法依赖每操作人工标注或蒙特卡洛续写回放，成本高且不实用。

Method: 离线训练操作条件下的记忆效用批评器；在线通过检索命中、引用和每次探测一次受控删除-重答来确定干预校准的条目级存在信用，并沿版本链传播为动作级代理奖励。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，8B本地策略达到77.5%，超越API教师（65.1%）及所有复现的外部系统，上下文仅为Mem0官方配置的八分之一；在LongMemEval上达到79.0%。消融实验表明增益来自因果校准而非信号密度。

Conclusion: 该策略收敛到多版本记忆组织，其收益是现有开环基线无法复现的，表明利用轨迹审计轨迹进行记忆操作信用分配是有效且可扩展的监督途径。

Abstract: Memory operations of long-horizon LLM agents are hard to supervise: an operation's value is unobservable when it is taken. But they are special -- they leave machine-readable evidence in the trajectory: retrieval hits and answer-time citations. Hindsight Memory-PRM exploits this audit trail twice: offline to train an operation-conditioned memory-utility critic, and online, where retrievals, citations, and one controlled deletion-and-reanswer per probe settle an intervention-calibrated entry-level presence credit, propagated along version chains as an action-level proxy reward -- no per-operation human labels, no Monte-Carlo replay of continuations. On held-out LoCoMo a local 8B policy reaches 77.5% under a fixed shared reader, surpassing its API teacher (65.1%) and all reproduced external systems, at one eighth the context of Mem0's official operating point; on LongMemEval, 79.0%. Ablations attribute the gain to causal calibration rather than signal density, and the policy converges to a multi-version memory organization whose gains no tested open-loop baseline reproduces.

</details>


### [175] [Agent Zero Memory: Provenance-Aware Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29606)
*Ming Wu,Pengyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agent Zero Memory 是一个基于来源感知的长期记忆系统，将用户对话、文件和连接源蒸馏为三个并行记忆子系统（事件时间线、实体-事件知识图谱、层级纪录片记忆），通过意图门控、路由器和三重并发智能检索实现引用锁定的阅读纪律，在 LongMemEval 和 LoCoMo 基准上分别达到 95.60% 和 93.60% 的最新水平，且质量主要由记忆驱动而非模型驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理记忆系统大多采用单一组织结构（事实存储、向量索引或知识图谱），每种结构都有其固有盲点，无法全面捕获用户和组织的历史信息。此外，传统的记忆系统缺乏来源追踪与引用锁定机制，难以保证记忆的忠实性和可验证性，容易产生幻觉或猜测性输出。

Method: 系统设计了三平行记忆系统：事件时间线（强调时间与变化的时序性）、实体-事件知识图谱（跨会话关联人物与项目）、层级纪录片记忆（持久事实的语义化、策展化与引用锁定）。检索流程包含意图门控（自包含回合零延迟）、来源路由器以及三个并发的代理性搜索，每个搜索对混合嵌入+词法检索执行工具使用循环，并受代理控制过滤。核心纪律是将每个学习记忆项携带来源、时间戳和证据指针，答案仅在引用锁定下生成，只能引用读者实际打开的证�证据，从结构上排除捏造。

Result: 在两个公开基准上取得新最先进成绩：LongMemEval 上 95.60%（比最强先前系统提高 +0.73），LoCoMo 上 93.60%（提高 +1.10）。在八个骨干 LLM 上的对照研究显示：准确率仅波动 3.4 个百分点，但单次查询成本差异约 30 倍，在成本低至 20 倍的条件下仍能达到接近最先进的质量，体现了记忆驱动而非模型驱动的质量特征。

Conclusion: Agent Zero Memory 通过多面记忆架构与严格的来源-引用纪律，在保持可验证性和可追溯性的同时实现了最先进的长期记忆性能，并证明通过精心设计的记忆系统可以有效降低对昂贵大模型的依赖，为构建忠实、持久、防捏造的 LLM 代理记忆提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents need durable, faithful memory of everything a user or organization has said and stored, yet most memory systems commit to a single organizing structure (a fact store, a vector index, or a knowledge graph) and inherit its blind spots. We present Agent Zero Memory, a provenance-aware long-term memory system that distils a user's conversations, files, and connected sources into three parallel memory systems, each capturing a different facet of the same history: an episodic Memory Events timeline that makes when and what changed first-class, an associative entity-event knowledge graph that links people and projects across sessions, and a semantic, curated, citation-locked Hierarchical Documentary Memory (HDM) of durable facts. A retrieval turn runs an intent gate (so self-contained turns add no latency), a source router, and three concurrent agentic searches, one per system, each a tool-using loop over hybrid (embedding + lexical) search under agent-controlled filters; their grounded, cited answers are integrated into one answer with a single confidence. We formalize the reading discipline: every learned item is a provenanced item carrying its origin, timestamp, and evidence pointer, and every answer is read under a citation lock, so it may cite only evidence its reader actually opened; fabrication is structurally excluded and the system abstains rather than guesses. On two public benchmarks the system sets a new state of the art: 95.60% on LongMemEval and 93.60% on LoCoMo, improving over the strongest prior systems by +0.73 and +1.10 points. A controlled study across eight backbone LLMs characterizes the accuracy-cost-latency frontier: accuracy varies by only 3.4 points while per-query cost varies by ~30x, with near-state-of-the-art quality at up to 20x lower cost per query, the signature of memory-driven, rather than model-driven, quality.

</details>


### [176] [Beyond Surface Alignment: Grounding the Dynamics of Situational Understanding and Generative Control in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29610)
*Chenghao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"接地对齐"（Grounded Alignment）框架，批判现有LLM对齐仅关注表面行为（流畅性、安全性、语调一致性），导致模型"风格自信但情境脆弱"。通过SitTest、ReCode、Branching Factor、Hindsight等工具诊断LLM在情境接地和生成接地中的失败，并提出Dynamic Control等动态控制方法（AI Realtor、Base-Aligned Model Collaboration、Annealed Sampling）来实现真正锚定于上下文和生成的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐范式主要优化LLM的表面行为（流畅、安全、语调一致），虽然适合日常对话，但这种表面对齐掩盖了模型缺乏真实情境理解的根本问题，使模型在风格上自信但在情境变化时表现脆弱。论文旨在超越表面对齐，建立模型与真实情境和生成过程之间的深层接地。

Method: 论文提出三层方法体系：(1) 情境接地评估——SitTest测试模型在动态环境中的"心智模型"保持能力，ReCode分析模型是否依赖深度句法依赖而非表面启发式；(2) 生成接地评估——引入Branching Factor (BF)映射生成空间，发现标准对齐调优导致过早的风格坍缩，Hindsight揭示模型不理解自身生成内容；(3) 动态控制——AI Realtor通过上下文工程补偿情境接地不足，Base-Aligned Model Collaboration解耦探索与风格约束，Annealed Sampling用于可验证强化学习，并应用于成瘾支持等高风险领域。

Result: 研究发现：SOTA模型即使在长上下文窗口下仍难以维持对环境的一致心智模型；模型依赖表面启发式而非深层句法依赖；标准对齐调优将生成分支空间压缩为过早的风格坍缩；模型常无法理解自己的生成输出。通过所提出的动态控制方法，可以在高风险领域实现更好的接地交互。

Conclusion: 对齐不应止步于表面行为，而应当追求接地对齐——即模型在输入理解（情境接地）和输出生成（生成接地）两个维度上都与人类需求和真实情境深度对齐。这项工作为超越表面对齐、构建情境和生成双重锚定的智能体提供了系统性的诊断工具和解决方案。

Abstract: The current alignment tuning paradigm for Large Language Models (LLMs) prioritizes surface-level behaviors -- fluency, safety, and tonal consistency. While effective for casual chat, this thesis argues that such surface alignment masks a lack of grounding, creating models that are stylistically confident but situationally brittle. We propose a framework of Grounded Alignment, analyzing how models process context (Input) and structure generation (Output), then aligning these grounded behaviors to human needs.
  First, we evaluate failures in Situational Grounding. SitTest shows that despite large context windows, state-of-the-art models struggle to maintain a consistent "mental model" of a changing environment. ReCode further shows that models rely on surface heuristics rather than deep syntactic dependencies: they "read" extensive histories without truly "understanding" the evolving situation.
  Second, we evaluate Generative Grounding. We introduce the Branching Factor (BF) to map LLM generation, finding that standard alignment tuning constricts this landscape into premature stylistic collapse. Hindsight further shows that models often fail to understand their own generations.
  Finally, we propose Dynamic Control for grounded interaction. AI Realtor demonstrates context engineering to compensate for poor situational grounding. Base-Aligned Model Collaboration decouples exploration from stylistic constraints. We also present Annealed Sampling for verifiable reinforcement learning and apply these ideas to Addiction Support, where model-generated rationalization offers a communication interface for high-stakes domains. Collectively, this work moves beyond surface alignment toward agents anchored in both context and generation.

</details>


### [177] [Cross-lingual Functional Vectors for Emotion Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29613)
*Jieying Xue,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Minh Le Nguyen,Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数向量（FVs）在跨语言任务中的可迁移性，以多语言多标签情感识别为基准，验证了FVs能够捕获语言无关的任务相关信号，并在干净和扰动零样本设置下显著提升跨语言任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明FVs能在结构化上下文学习场景中恢复任务行为，但其在语义复杂任务上的有效性以及跨语言泛化能力仍未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以多语言多标签情感识别作为语义分类基准，从源语言提取FVs，在干净与扰动的零样本设置下（不提供推理演示）检验其能否在不同目标语言中引导任务行为。同时分析不同LLM构建有效FVs所对应的注意力头最佳范围及其跨语言一致性。

Result: 在多种跨语言设置下，应用FVs均显著提升了性能，证明FVs捕获的是语言无关的任务相关信号；每个LLM存在相对稳定的最优注意力头范围来构建有效FVs，且该模式跨语言一致；FVs能部分复现少样本上下文学习的任务引导效果，同时避免了处理多个演示的计算开销。

Conclusion: FVs是一种轻量级且可迁移的多语言任务适配机制，在实用的大规模应用场景中具有有效性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Function vectors (FVs) have recently emerged as a promising mechanism for steering the behavior of large language models (LLMs) by injecting task-specific latent direction representations derived from in-context demonstrations. While prior studies have shown that FVs can recover task behavior in structured in-context learning settings, their effectiveness on semantically complex tasks and their ability to generalize across languages remain underexplored. We investigate the cross-lingual transferability of FVs using multilingual multi-label emotion recognition as a challenging semantic classification benchmark. Specifically, we examine whether FVs extracted from a source language can steer task behavior in another language under both standard clean and perturbed zero-shot settings without providing demonstrations during inference. Across diverse cross-lingual settings, applying FVs substantially improves performance, suggesting that FVs capture language-agnostic, task-relevant signals rather than purely language-specific lexical patterns, and highlighting their potential as a lightweight and transferable mechanism for multilingual task adaptation. We observe that each LLM exhibits a relatively stable optimal range of attention heads for constructing effective FVs, and the pattern remains consistent across languages. In addition, FVs can partially replicate the task-steering effects of standard few-shot in-context learning while avoiding the computational overhead of processing multiple demonstrations, making them effective for large-scale practical applications. Our code is available at https://github.com/yingjie7/cross_lingual_fvs.

</details>


### [178] [JPO: Juris Policy Optimization for Structured Legal Reasoning in Criminal Judgment Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29616)
*Zhaolu Kang,Yantao Liu,Tailong Luo,Leqi Zheng,Lei Wei,Chenghua Zhu,Junhao Gong,Jiachen Qian,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Jiaxin Liu,Yuan Wang,Hao Zhang,Zixia Wang,Rong Fu,Zheng Lin,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Juris Policy Optimization (JPO)，一种面向中文刑事判决预测的结构化法律推理后训练框架，通过四步推理流程监督、复合奖励强化学习以及token级优势重加权与自适应裁剪，在三个中文法律基准上显著提升判决预测与推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有刑事判决预测方法主要优化最终标签，即使尝试评估推理质量也是间接的，常依赖LLM生成的rubrics，反映的是模型内部偏好而非法律裁判本身固有的逻辑结构（法条-事实匹配、罪名-法条证成、量刑-罪名一致）。

Method: JPO包含两个阶段：(1) 使用教师模型生成的rationales监督标准化的四步推理过程；(2) 应用强化学习，使用由法律预测质量、推理结构完整性和跨步骤一致性组成的复合奖励。此外引入token级优势重加权和自适应裁剪，以针对法律上显著的推理片段进行精细优化。

Result: 在多个开源语言模型和三个中文法律基准上的实验表明，JPO在判决预测和推理质量方面均一致优于有监督微调和强化学习基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模法律裁判的结构化推理逻辑而非仅优化最终标签，JPO能够有效提升模型在刑事判决预测中的判决准确性与推理质量，验证了结构化推理后训练范式在法律领域的有效性。

Abstract: Criminal judgment prediction requires models to infer statutory articles, charges, and sentencing outcomes from case facts. Unlike standard classification tasks, it involves a structured reasoning process in which statutes should be matched with facts, charges should be justified by statutes, and sentencing outcomes should remain consistent with charges. Existing approaches optimize final labels, and while some have attempted to evaluate reasoning quality, their evaluations are indirect, often relying on LLM-generated rubrics that reflect model-internal preferences rather than the inherent logical structure of legal adjudication. We propose Juris Policy Optimization (JPO), a post-training framework for structured legal reasoning in Chinese criminal judgment prediction. JPO first uses teacher-generated rationales to supervise a standardized four-step reasoning process, and then applies reinforcement learning with a composite reward over legal prediction quality, reasoning structure completeness, and cross-step consistency. JPO further introduces token-level advantage reweighting and adaptive clipping for legally salient reasoning segments. Experiments on multiple open-source language models and three Chinese legal benchmarks show that JPO consistently improves both judgment prediction and reasoning quality over supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Memory-First Fact-Checking: A Knowledge-Graph-Grounded Multi-Agent System for Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29617)
*Amelia Petrenciuc,Alexandru Lecu,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合事实验证框架，将基于知识图谱的语义记忆与对抗性多智能体推理相结合，用于可解释的虚假信息检测。系统采用"记忆优先、网页回退"架构，通过Sentence-BERT语义检索和自然语言推理在双索引知识图谱中评估输入声明；当图谱证据不足时，从可信网络来源收集信息，并由支持、反驳和裁判三种对抗性智能体组成的法庭进行评估。图感知置信机制综合语义相似度、NLI置信度和结构图证据来决定内部知识是否充分。验证后的信息被转化为结构化三元组并加入知识图谱，实现语义记忆的增量扩展。在COVID-19虚假信息基准评测中，该框架在已解决声明上达到97.4%的准确率和92.6%的宏平均F1分数，优于Llama 3.3 70B基线（准确率87.7%，F1为86.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测方法存在两个主要不足：一是纯LLM推理缺乏可解释性和可验证的知识依据，容易产生幻觉；二是知识图谱方法在知识覆盖不足时难以做出可靠判断。因此需要一种既能利用结构化知识提供可解释依据，又能在知识不足时灵活回退到外部证据的混合框架。

Method: 提出混合事实验证框架，核心包括：(1) 双索引知识图谱，结合Sentence-BERT语义检索与NLI进行声明评估；(2) 图感知置信机制，综合语义相似度、NLI置信度和结构图证据判断内部知识充分性；(3) 对抗性智能体法庭（支持、矛盾、裁判三智能体）对网络证据进行多角度审查；(4) 记忆增量机制，将验证后的信息转化为三元组回写知识图谱。

Result: 在自建的COVID-19虚假信息基准上，框架在已解决声明上达到97.4%准确率和92.6%宏平均F1分数，显著优于Llama 3.3 70B基线（准确率87.7%，F1分数86.3%），提升幅度分别约为9.7个百分点的准确率和6.3个百分点的F1分数。

Conclusion: 混合知识图谱与对抗性多智能体推理的架构能够有效兼顾虚假信息检测的准确性和可解释性。记忆优先、网页回退的策略既减少了不必要的网络检索开销，又通过持续的知识回写实现了系统语义记忆的增量扩展，展示了知识图谱在增强LLM事实核查能力方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a hybrid fact-checking framework that integrates Knowledge Graph-based semantic memory with adversarial multi-agent reasoning for explainable misinformation detection. The proposed system follows a memory-first, web-fallback architecture, in which input claims are initially evaluated against a dual-index Knowledge Graph through Sentence-BERT-based semantic retrieval and Natural Language Inference. When the evidence retrieved from the graph is insufficient to support a reliable decision, the framework collects information from trusted web sources and assesses it using an adversarial tribunal composed of support, contradiction, and judging agents. A graph-aware confidence mechanism combines semantic similarity, NLI confidence, and structural graph evidence to determine whether internal knowledge is sufficient, thereby reducing unnecessary web retrieval. Following verification, validated information is transformed into structured triples and incorporated into the Knowledge Graph, supporting the incremental expansion of the system's semantic memory. Experimental evaluation on a curated COVID-19 misinformation benchmark demonstrates that the proposed framework achieves an accuracy of 97.4\% and a macro-averaged F1-score of 92.6% on resolved claims, outperforming a Llama~3.3~70B baseline, which obtains an accuracy of 87.7% and a macro-averaged F1-score of 86.3%.

</details>


### [180] [MI-Distillation: Selecting from Model-Interpolated Instruct-Reasoning Data Spectrum for Chain-of-Thought Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29623)
*Yangsong Lan,Renkai Hu,HongKai Zheng,Bo Zhang,Renzhi Wang,Hongliang Dai,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从梯度视角分析了长思维链（Long CoT）蒸馏到小模型效果不佳的原因，发现Long CoT会导致更大的梯度幅度和更集中的更新方向，且该现象随学生模型容量增大而加剧。据此提出MI-Distillation框架和SeqLSS评分方法，通过模型插值构建连续的数据谱系并选择信息量大且可学习性强的推理轨迹，显著提升了小模型的CoT蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过长思维链推理在复杂问题上表现优异，但将其蒸馏到小模型时，直接的Long CoT监督收益有限，甚至不如简洁的Short CoT rationale。作者希望从梯度角度理解这一现象的根本原因，并据此设计更有效的蒸馏方法。

Method: 1) 从梯度中心视角分析Long CoT与Short CoT蒸馏的差异，揭示梯度幅度和更新方向集中度的规律；2) 提出MI-Distillation框架，通过模型插值构建连续的Instruct-Reasoning数据谱系；3) 引入SeqLSS（序列可学习惊异度评分），在谱系中筛选既信息丰富又对学生模型可学习的推理轨迹用于蒸馏。

Result: 在多个推理基准上的大量实验表明，MI-Distillation相比强大的Long CoT基线方法，在小模型的CoT蒸馏上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 有效的小模型Long CoT蒸馏需要在推理轨迹的信息密度与分布对齐（相对于学生模型）之间取得平衡；MI-Distillation通过构建数据谱系并智能选择轨迹来实现这一平衡，为长思维链知识的迁移提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have shown strong performance on complex problems through long chain-of-thought (Long CoT) reasoning. However, distilling such trajectories into smaller student models remains challenging: direct Long CoT supervision often provides limited gains and can be less effective than concise Short CoT rationales. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon from a gradient-centric perspective. Our analysis shows that Long CoT induces larger gradient magnitudes and more concentrated update directions than Short CoT, with this effect becoming more pronounced as student model capacity increases. These findings suggest that effective Long CoT distillation requires balancing the reasoning information density of reasoning trajectories with their distributional alignment to the student model. Motivated by this insight, we propose \textbf{M}odel \textbf{I}nterporlation \textbf{Distillation} (\textbf{MI-Distillation}), a framework that constructs a continuous Instruct-Reasoning data spectrum through model interpolation. To select suitable trajectories from this spectrum, we further introduce \textbf{Seq}uential \textbf{L}earnable \textbf{S}urprisal \textbf{S}core (\textbf{SeqLSS}), which favors reasoning paths that are both informative and learnable for the student. Extensive experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that MI-Distillation consistently improves small model CoT distillation over strong Long CoT baselines.

</details>


### [181] [PrivBench: A Holistic and Modular Benchmarking Platform for Evaluating Text-to-Text Privatization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29624)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Ahmet Bilal Akın,Alexandra Klymenko,Jana Diesner,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PrivBench，一个用于自然语言处理中文本到文本隐私化任务评估的综合性模块化基准测试平台，旨在统一和标准化隐私化方法的评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到文本隐私化方法在NLP领域取得进展，但其评估缺乏统一标准，现有文献使用多种技术和指标来量化隐私保护能力，导致结果难以比较和复现。作者旨在通过统一的评估框架解决这一问题。

Method: PrivBench是一个整体性与模块化相结合的基准测试平台，通过一系列定义的desiderata（期望属性）来评估隐私化方法，这些desiderata被组织成模块；平台具有可扩展性以支持未来更新和基准版本，并采用实时评估和公开排行榜来促进用户参与和竞争。

Result: PrivBench平台已实现并对公众免费开放，提供实时评估和在线公开排行榜功能，供研究人员和从业者使用。

Conclusion: PrivBench通过提供统一、模块化、可扩展且以用户为中心的评估平台，为文本到文本隐私化的标准化评估做出了贡献，能够促进该领域的系统性比较和持续改进。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing methods have enabled novel solutions and advances in the field of privacy, particularly in the sub-domain of text-to-text privatization, where the goal is to transform a sensitive input text into a privatized output by ideally masking (in)directly identifiable or otherwise private information. The evaluation of text-to-text privatization, however, is not straightforward, and the extant literature has utilized a myriad of techniques and metrics to quantify the privacy-preserving capabilities of privatization methods. Seeking to unify the evaluation of text-to-text privatization, we introduce PrivBench, a holistic and modular benchmarking platform for researchers and practitioners working on text privatization. PrivBench is holistic in that it evaluates privatization on a series of defined desiderata, which are structured into modules. PrivBench is not only modular but also extensible, allowing for future updates and benchmark versions. PrivBench is user-centered and promotes competition via real-time evaluation and a live public leaderboard. The platform is free to use and openly accessible at https://privbench.com/.

</details>


### [182] [ACTD: Anchor-Based Cross-Tokenizer Distillation with Residual Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29662)
*Huiyi Zhang,Zijian Li,Xiaocheng Feng,Weitao Ma,Xiaoliang Yang,Yichong Huang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于锚点的跨分词器知识蒸馏方法（ACTD），通过词汇和序列对齐解决跨模型家族蒸馏中的词汇/序列错位问题，并引入锚点损失与残差正则化来抑制对齐噪声，在多教师设置下进一步扩展，在五个推理基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏能有效地将大语言模型的推理能力迁移到轻量级学生模型，但跨分词器蒸馏因词汇表与序列错位而面临挑战，且近似词汇对齐会引入额外噪声，影响蒸馏效果。

Method: ACTD通过词汇对齐和序列对齐桥接结构异质性；提出新颖的锚点损失（anchor loss）配合残差正则化（residual regularization）来缓解对齐噪声；并将框架扩展到多教师蒸馏场景。

Result: 在五个推理基准上、使用三个不同的教师模型进行评估，ACTD达到最先进性能；其多教师扩展版本优于最强的单教师和多教师基线，验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ACTD有效解决了跨分词器蒸馏中的词汇与序列错位及对齐噪声问题，在多教师场景下同样表现出色，为跨模型家族的知识迁移提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation effectively transfers reasoning capabilities from large language models to lightweight student models. To enable knowledge transfer across disparate model families, researchers increasingly explore cross-tokenizer distillation. However, cross-tokenizer distillation remains challenging due to vocabulary and sequence misalignment, while approximate vocabulary alignment can introduce additional noise into distillation. To address these challenges, we propose Anchor-Based Cross-Tokenizer Distillation with Residual Regularization (ACTD). ACTD bridges structural heterogeneity through vocabulary and sequence alignment, while mitigating alignment noise via a novel anchor loss with residual regularization. We further extend this framework to a multi-teacher setting. Evaluated across five reasoning benchmarks with three distinct teacher models, ACTD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Moreover, its multi-teacher extension outperforms the strongest single-teacher and multi-teacher baselines, further demonstrating the robustness of our method.

</details>


### [183] [A Hub of Short Rows Inflates Intrinsic Dimension Estimation of Token Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29702)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现词元嵌入表中的"中心簇"(hub)会严重偏置最近邻内在维度(ID)估计器的结果。移除这些hub行后，11个模型(从GPT-2到GLM-4.7)的ID估计从重尾分布和随参数增长的数值(27到122)坍缩到狭窄范围(10到17)。该现象在数百行hub行即可完全破坏估计，且行归一化可达到与移除相同的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前实验声称Pythia等模型的词元嵌入表内在维度随模型参数规模增长(160M到12B时ID从27增至122)，本文旨在揭示这一结论是虚假的，源于嵌入表中靠近原点的"hub"行对最近邻ID估计器的系统性偏差，进而纠正对大规模语言模型嵌入结构特性的认知。

Method: 作者通过分析token嵌入表在原点附近的短行簇(即hub)如何影响TwoNN等最近邻ID估计器：由于测度集中效应，任何token到中心簇的距离都小于到其他token的距离，导致前两个最近邻几乎等距地落入hub行，从而夸大ID估计。通过移除hub行或对行进行归一化，重新测量11个模型的嵌入表ID，并对比去除前后以及不同参数规模下的结果。

Result: 移除hub后，重尾分布消失，ID从原本随参数增长的27-122范围坍缩到所有模型一致的10-17狭窄区间。Hub只需数百行即可完全膨胀ID估计。与"欠训练token检测器"的假设相反，Pythia的hub行在训练中持续更新，其本质特征是行长度而非缺乏更新。归一化行向量与移除hub效果相同。

Conclusion: 先前关于嵌入表ID随模型规模增长的结论是hub伪影造成的假象。嵌入表的内在维度实际上与模型规模无关，稳定在10-17的窄范围内。Hub问题可通过移除或归一化解决，且行长度是区分hub行的关键特征。这一发现修正了对大语言模型嵌入空间结构的理解，并为ID估计在嵌入表上的可靠应用提供了方法学指导。

Abstract: A token-embedding table holds a hub of short rows near its origin, and we show that this cluster biases what nearest-neighbor intrinsic-dimension (ID) estimators report. Because of the concentration of measure, a token is closer to the central cluster than to any other token, so its first two neighbors are both hub rows at nearly the same distance. As a result, the ID estimators such as TwoNN return a dimension far above the real ID. Measured one token at a time, dimension is a heavy-tailed distribution. Measured on the full vocabulary, it grows with the model's parameter count. However, when we remove the hub, the heavy tail disappears and the measured dimension collapses to a narrow range for eleven models, from GPT-2 to models such as K3 and GLM-4.7. The hub acts as a switch: a few hundred rows are enough to fully inflate the estimate. We reproduced an experiment stating that the intrinsic dimension (ID) of Pythia's token-embedding table grows with the parameter count, from $27$ to $122$ between 160M and 12B parameters. We show that this result disappears when the hub is removed: the table then reads $10$ to $17$ at every size. The hub contains a subset of the population that under-trained-token detectors flag, but on Pythia the hub that we detected and removed as a whole was updated during training: what seem to characterize these rows is simply their length, not an absence of updates. Finally, we show that normalizing the rows instead of removing them gives the same lower reading.

</details>


### [184] [The Depth Flow of Token Representations Is Nonlinear and Does Not Descend Its Own Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29706)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过将语言模型token表示在层间传播的轨迹建模为离散Langevin方程，拟合其运动方程并分析动力系统的结构特性，发现流不是线性的，且同时包含势降和旋转分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型内部token表征如何随层数演变，探索用简单动力学模型（如Langevin方程）刻画此流动的可行性，并揭示流动背后更精细的结构特性（密度、势、旋转）。

Method: 对Pythia-160M和Pythia-410M的语料均值轨迹，用离散Langevin模型拟合token表征流的运动方程，对比线性映射与二次漂移项的预测得分，并用Kramers-Moyal估计验证；进一步分析漂移是否对应log密度的梯度下降、以及旋转分量的占比与token性质（角秩、范数秩、浓度秩）随层数的变化。

Result: 在每一层过渡中，二次漂移项均优于线性映射；漂移下降的势不等于流的log密度；旋转分量占可解释漂移的4%-45%，且token的角秩在全部十三层间保持稳定，而范数秩被打乱、浓度秩被最后一层逆转。

Conclusion: 语言模型中token表征的层间流动具有非线性的动力学结构，包含了势降与显著的旋转分量，token的不同几何属性（角、范数、浓度）在流动中展现不同的演化模式。

Abstract: A token's representation is carried through the network layer by layer. The whole vocabulary carried together forms a flow. We fit this flow's equation of motion as a discrete Langevin model over corpus-mean trajectories of Pythia-160M and Pythia-410M, and score the predicted steps on held-out tokens. Linear maps are often used as cheap surrogates for a layer. The flow they summarize is not linear: a quadratic drift beats the linear linear map at every transition of both models, and the Kramers--Moyal estimator agrees wherever its neighborhoods stay local.
  We then characterize the flow further. First, we show that it does not descend its own log-density. The drift instead descends a potential that is not the density. Second, the rotational component is not negligible, $4$ to $45\%$ of the explainable drift, and the circulation shows in what the flow preserves: a token keeps its angular rank across all thirteen layers while its norm rank is shuffled and its concentration rank is reversed by the last block.

</details>


### [185] [DVBench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Understanding Dynamic Charts and Narratives in Data Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29711)
*Bomiao Wang,Zekai Shao,Jiexiang Lan,Xiaoliang Fu,Xingchen Zeng,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DVBench基准，专门用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在数据视频理解上的能力。DVBench包含300个真实世界数据视频和1,000个经过人工验证的QA对，将数据视频理解分解为五个维度。评估9个MLLM后发现Gemini-3.1-Pro表现最佳，Kimi-k2.5是最强的开源模型，并揭示了两个有趣现象：开源模型性能不与参数量严格成正比，叙事能力不保证视觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估将图表理解与视频理解割裂开来，忽视了时间演化下结构化视觉信息的理解需求。数据视频作为一种将动态图表与结构化叙事相结合的媒介，其理解能力尚未得到系统评估，存在关键的研究空白。

Method: 将数据视频理解分解为五个维度；构建包含300个真实世界数据视频和1,000个高质量QA对的数据集，通过严格的半自动化流程进行数据清洗与人工验证；对9种MLLM进行系统化评估，并进行细粒度分析与消融实验（包括帧配置和字幕输入的影响）。

Result: Gemini-3.1-Pro在整体性能上表现最优，Kimi-k2.5是最强的开源模型。发现两个重要现象：一是开源模型性能不与参数量严格呈正比；二是叙事能力并不保证视觉理解能力。细粒度分析和消融实验揭示了各维度上的模型弱点以及帧配置、字幕输入对性能的影响。

Conclusion: DVBench填补了数据视频理解评估的空白，系统揭示了当前MLLM在该任务上的能力分布与不足。研究发现为未来的MLLM开发提供了重要的方向性指导，尤其是关于模型规模与能力关系、多模态能力解耦等关键问题的启示。

Abstract: While MLLMs have made significant strides in chart comprehension and video understanding, current evaluations largely isolate these capabilities, leaving a critical gap in understanding temporally evolving structured visual information. To address this gap, we introduce DVBench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on data videos, a storytelling medium that integrates dynamic charts with structured narratives. We decompose data video understanding into five dimensions. DVBench comprises 300 real-world data videos and 1,000 human-verified QA pairs curated through a rigorous semi-automated pipeline. Extensive evaluations of nine MLLMs show that Gemini-3.1-Pro achieves the best overall performance, while Kimi-k2.5 is the strongest open-source model. We further identify two notable phenomena: open-source model performance does not scale strictly with parameter size, and narrative proficiency does not guarantee visual capability. Fine-grained analyses and ablation studies further reveal dimension-specific weaknesses and the effects of frame configurations and subtitle inputs, informing future MLLM development. DVBench is publicly available at https://bomiaowang.github.io/DVBench/.

</details>


### [186] [Evaluating the Capabilities of LLMs for Persuasive Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29738)
*Jordan Robinson,Angus R. Williams,Katie Atkinson,Anthony G. Cohn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Persuasio——一个基于形式论证理论的多智能体对话平台，用于在自由文本辩论中裁决逻辑胜负。通过192场人类与LLM之间的英国政治议题辩论，结合自动化裁决和9,702个众包成对评判，发现LLM在主观说服力排名中占据主导，但在形式论证裁决中表现显著较差，二者存在系统性脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成看似极具说服力的文本，但"听起来有说服力"是否等同于"论证得好"这一问题尚未被系统检验。现有研究多依赖主观感知评估说服力，缺乏基于形式论证理论对逻辑胜负进行客观判定的机制，因此需要构建一个能在自由文本辩论场景下区分主观说服力与形式论证强度的评测平台。

Method: 作者构建了Persuasio多智能体对话平台，采用基于形式论证论辩理论的裁判机制为自由文本辩论判定逻辑胜负。利用该平台生成了192场关于英国政治议题的人类与LLM辩论，并对22个对话参与者进行评测。评测方式包括自动化论证裁决以及1,386个标注实例上的9,702个众包成对评判。实验中还对比了多智能体变体和检索增强变体。

Result: 研究发现主观说服力与形式说服力之间存在一致的解耦现象：LLM在主观排名中占据主导地位，但在基于论证理论的裁决下表现明显更差，人类参与者仍具有竞争力。多智能体和检索增强变体进一步扩大了这一差距。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在说服性对话中修辞流畅性与形式论证强度之间的系统性鸿沟，表明LLM擅长产出令人信服的话语，但在严格的逻辑论证裁决下并未展现出相应的论证能力，这为我们理解和评估LLM的说服能力提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate apparently highly persuasive text, but does sounding persuasive mean arguing well? We introduce \textsc{Persuasio}, a multi-agent dialogue platform grounded in a formal argumentation-based theory of persuasion dialogues that adjudicates logical winners during free-text debates. Using this system, we generated 192 debates on a UK political topic between humans and LLMs, and evaluated 22 interlocutors through both automated adjudication and 9,702 crowdsourced pairwise judgements across 1{,}386 annotation instances. We observed a consistent decoupling between subjective and formal persuasiveness: LLMs dominated the subjective ranking yet performed substantially worse under argumentation-theoretic adjudication, where humans remained competitive. Multi-agent and retrieval-augmented variants further widened this divergence. These findings reveal a systematic gap between rhetorical fluency and formal argumentative strength in LLM-based persuasive dialogues.

</details>


### [187] [ReTrace: Rejected-Trajectory Conditioning for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29748)
*Luxi Lin,Zhanpeng Zeng,Shuang Peng,Songwei Liu,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTrace，一种基于拒绝轨迹条件化的投机解码方法。它利用被拒绝的草案后缀中仍可能包含与目标模型延续对齐的有用信息，将这些被拒绝token的隐藏表示跨轮次传递给下一轮草案生成，通过门控残差融合注入drafter的输入嵌入，在不改变目标端验证过程、不增加额外前向传播的前提下，提升平均接受长度和端到端解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准投机解码中，前缀式验证在第一次拒绝后就丢弃剩余草案后缀，这些位置上花费的生成和验证计算未贡献于解码进展。作者发现即使草案token在局部被拒绝，被拒绝的后缀仍可能与目标延续对齐，包含有价值的语义和结构信息，值得在后续轮次中加以利用。

Method: ReTrace的核心是跨轮次条件化：将上一轮被拒绝后缀的隐藏表示保留下来，与下一轮草案块对齐，利用同一验证过程中的目标感知校正信号进行精炼，再通过门控残差融合将其融入drafter的输入嵌入中。该方法不修改轮内提案生成，因此与现有草案改进方法正交。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和开放对话等任务上与Qwen3模型进行的实验表明，ReTrace在平均接受长度和端到端解码速度上均持续优于其DFlash基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入跨轮次条件化而不修改轮内提案生成流程，ReTrace保持了投机解码的无损性质（被拒绝token永不提交、目标验证不变、无需额外模型前向），且与现有草案改进方法正交，可进一步组合以获得更大收益。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive language model inference by having a lightweight draft model propose multiple candidate tokens, which are then verified in parallel by a larger target model. However, after the first rejection, standard prefix-based verification discards the remaining draft suffix, so the computation spent generating and verifying those positions does not contribute to decoding progress. Focusing on DFlash, we show that rejected positions in a rejected suffix may still align with the target continuation, indicating that the draft model can retain useful semantic and structural information despite local token-level errors. Motivated by this observation and inspired by conditional diffusion, we introduce~\textbf{ReTrace}, a rejected-trajectory conditioning method that conditions each draft block on the rejected suffix from the previous round rather than generating it from fresh mask placeholders alone. ReTrace retains the hidden representations of the rejected suffixes, aligns them with the next draft block, refines them using target-aware correction signals from the same verification pass, and admits them into the drafter's input embeddings through gated residual fusion. Because rejected tokens are never committed and target-side verification remains unchanged, ReTrace preserves the lossless property of speculative decoding without requiring an additional model forward pass. Experiments with Qwen3 models across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and open-ended dialogue demonstrate that ReTrace consistently improves average acceptance length and end-to-end decoding speed over its DFlash backbone. By introducing cross-round conditioning without modifying within-round proposal generation, ReTrace is largely orthogonal to existing drafting improvements and might be combined with them for further gains.

</details>


### [188] [HiVe: Beyond Static Prompts for Multitask Learning via Hierarchy-based Vertical Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29790)
*HyeonJik Bae,Minyeol Kim,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiVe是一个多层级提示调优框架，通过利用任务间关系构建提示层次结构，并在推理时采用垂直混合专家（V-MoE）机制实现输入依赖的提示特化，在多样任务上优于强提示调优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示调优方法要么使用扁平提示结构，要么使用固定提示组合的分层结构，限制了提示的自适应特化能力。作者希望解决这一局限性，实现更灵活的输入依赖的提示组成。

Method: HiVe框架包含两个关键组件：（1）训练阶段利用任务间关系构建提示层次结构（prompt hierarchy）；（2）推理阶段采用垂直混合专家（V-MoE）机制，根据每个输入所需的特化程度动态组合不同层级的提示。

Result: 实验表明HiVe在多样化任务上持续优于强提示调优基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过多层级提示建模和输入依赖的特化组合，HiVe有效提升了提示调优的适应性和性能，为PEFT提供了一种更灵活的分层提示方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to scale, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become a practical alternative to full-parameter adaptation. Prompt tuning is effective, but existing approaches either use flat prompt structures or hierarchical structures with fixed prompt composition, limiting adaptive prompt specialization. To address this limitation, we propose HiVe, a prompt tuning framework that models prompts at multiple levels and enables input-dependent specialization. HiVe constructs a prompt hierarchy by leveraging inter-task relationships during training, and employs a vertical mixture-of-experts (V-MoE) mechanism at inference time to compose prompts up to the level of specialization required for each input. Experiments show that HiVe consistently outperforms strong prompt tuning baselines across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [189] [R$^2$A: Learning Persona Policies Through Persona Representation Learning and Runtime Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29798)
*Mohan Zhang,Chengsong You,Xiaoyu Cao,Zhen Sun,Xiaohan Jia,Junwei Zhou,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona Selection--Realization框架，将人格行为生成分解为Persona Selection（人格选择）和Persona Realization（人格实现）两个组件，并据此提出R²A两阶段方法（Persona Representation Learning + Persona Runtime Alignment），在12个评估设置上优于基座模型和静态人格引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的人格行为在不同任务上下文中可能产生不同效果，静态人格引导（static Persona elicitation）在不同任务上表现不稳定。现有方法缺乏对人格策略中"选择什么行为"与"如何实现该行为"两个环节的显式建模与优化。

Method: 1) 定义Persona Selection--Realization框架，引入潜变量人格状态，将行为生成分解为Persona Selection和Persona Realization，并据此定义Selection Gap和Realization Gap；2) 提出R²A两阶段方法：Persona Representation Learning阶段使用Who--How--What结构化表示编码目标人格的目标、条件行为原则和轨迹级表现；Persona Runtime Alignment阶段移除显式人格规范，利用任务反馈联合校准行为选择与轨迹实现。

Result: 在覆盖Accountable-Professional人格四条原则的12个评估设置中，R²A整体优于基座模型和静态人格引导。消融实验表明，Persona Representation Learning对防止Runtime Alignment产生行为失衡策略以及实现更稳定的人格策略学习至关重要。

Conclusion: 人格策略不应是静态的，而应通过显式分解人格选择与实现两个环节，并借助任务反馈进行动态校准，才能在不同上下文中实现一致且平衡的人格行为表现。

Abstract: The same Persona behavior can be beneficial in one context but harmful in another, causing static Persona elicitation to perform inconsistently across tasks. We introduce the Persona Selection--Realization Framework, which models behavior generation through a latent Persona state and decomposes it into Persona Selection and Persona Realization. The discrepancies between static Persona elicitation and an ideal Persona policy in these two components define the Selection Gap and Realization Gap, respectively. Building on this framework, we propose R$^2$A, a two-stage approach for learning Persona policies. Persona Representation Learning uses structured Who--How--What presentations to encode the target Persona's objective, conditional behavioral principles, and trajectory-level manifestations. Persona Runtime Alignment then removes the explicit Persona specification and jointly calibrates behavior selection and trajectory realization using task feedback. Across 12 evaluation settings covering the four principles of the Accountable-Professional Persona studied in this work, R$^2$A overall outperforms both the base model and static Persona elicitation. Ablation results further show that Persona Representation Learning is critical for preventing Runtime Alignment from producing behaviorally imbalanced policies and for achieving more stable Persona policy learning.

</details>


### [190] [A^2Agent: Action-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Repository-Level Code Localization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29831)
*Doyeon Kim,Suyoung Bae,Yumin Lee,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种动作感知（action-aware）的强化学习方法，通过每轮奖励序列和动作级优势估计来增强代码问题定位，在SWE-Bench数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖稀疏的轨迹级信号，无法识别哪些单轮动作是有效的，且常出现"探索时发现正确代码区域但未能提交"的问题，导致最终定位失败。

Method: 1) 提出每轮（per-turn）奖励序列，同时奖励金代码区域的发现与提交；2) 设计动作级优势估计（action-level advantage estimation）方案，通过将共享相同探索上下文的轮次分组来隔离每个动作的信用分配（credit assignment）。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Verified上平均F1比SOTA提升1.58%，在SWE-Bench Pro上提升8.55%，且4B参数的模型优于高达8倍规模的基线模型。

Conclusion: 动作感知的强化学习能够有效解决稀疏奖励下的信用分配问题，提升代码问题定位的准确性与稳定性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Localizing issue-relevant code regions is a critical step in automated software engineering. However, due to their reliance on sparse trajectory-level signals, existing methods cannot identify which per-turn actions are effective and often discover correct code regions during exploration but fail to commit them. To address these limitations, we propose an action-aware reinforcement learning method that combines a per-turn reward sequence rewarding both the discovery and commitment of gold code regions with an action-level advantage estimation scheme that isolates each action's credit by grouping turns sharing the same exploration context. Extensive evaluations show that our method improves the average F1 over the state-of-the-art (SOTA) by 1.58% on SWE-Bench Verified and 8.55% on SWE-Bench Pro, with our 4B model outperforming baselines up to 8x larger. Our code is available at https://github.com/donian00/A2Agent.

</details>


### [191] [You Know What I Mean: A Benchmark for Agentic Conversational Reference Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29834)
*Karen Fuchs,Uri Katz,Yoav Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出会话引用接地（CoRG）问题，即使用给定工具集在对话中解析模糊引用到外部工作区中的唯一目标。作者构建了RepoRef基准，包含92个仓库中400个开发者聊天片段，实验表明即使最佳智能体成功率仅为67.0%，证明CoRG对当前代理而言仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作对话中经常出现间接引用（如"这看起来像昨天讨论的修复方案"），解析这类引用需要结合对话上下文和通过API或用户界面可访问的外部工作区证据。现有研究缺乏对此类问题的正式定义和系统评估。

Method: 形式化定义CoRG问题，并构建RepoRef基准数据集——包含92个GitHub仓库中400个开发者聊天片段，数据来源覆盖GitHub issues、pull requests和commits。智能体需通过多步工具调用来搜索、检查元数据和内容、排除相近候选来解析引用。

Result: CoRG需要多步工具使用（不同于单次检索任务）。当前最优智能体仅达到67.0%的成功率，仍有三分之一的引用无法解析，表明该任务对现有智能体具有显著挑战性。

Conclusion: CoRG可作为评估智能体在真实多工具环境中搜索、检查和验证信息能力的基准，为研究复杂工具使用和引用解析提供具体测试平台。

Abstract: Collaborative conversations frequently contain references whose targets are indirect rather than named: resolving "this looks like the fix discussed yesterday" requires combining conversational context with evidence from the surrounding workspace which is accessible through APIs or user interfaces. We formalize this problem as Conversational Reference Grounding (CoRG): using a given set of tools to resolve a reference in conversation to the unique external item intended by the speaker. CoRG is challenging because it combines lexical, semantic, and temporal cues distributed across the conversation and the external workspace. Agents must translate these heterogeneous signals into effective tool use: formulating strategies, discovering plausible candidates, inspecting their metadata and content, and ruling out close alternatives. We study CoRG through RepoRef, a benchmark of 400 developer-chat segments grounded in GitHub issues, pull requests, and commits across 92 repositories. Unlike single-shot retrieval tasks, RepoRef often requires multi-step tool use. Our results show that CoRG remains challenging for current agents, even the best agent reaches only 67.0% success rate, leaving one third of references unresolved. These findings position CoRG as a concrete benchmark for studying how agents search, inspect, and verify information in realistic multi-tool environments.

</details>


### [192] [EVAR: Evidence-Validated Hypothesis Admission for Budget-Aware Narrative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29835)
*Peilin Liu,Zhiquan Ji,Jinglong Ping*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EVAR框架，一种基于证据验证的假设准入机制，用于预算感知的叙事推理。EVAR将长篇叙事编译为不可变的证据存储库，在推理过程中对候选假设进行验证后再纳入推理轨迹，过滤掉无证据支持的中间假设，从而提升推理的忠实性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在非交互式长篇幅叙事上进行推理时，常常产生流畅但缺乏证据支撑的结论。核心失败模式是：无依据的中间假设进入推理轨迹后污染后续推断，尤其当证据分散在故事的不同部分时更为严重。

Method: EVAR包含三个关键模块：1) 将叙事编译为不可变的证据存储库（source-linked原子声明）；2) 根据未解决的空缺与不确定性信号分配实例特定的推理预算；3) 在优化阶段直接提出候选假设，构造基于假设的验证挑战，在纳入前对照锁定的证据库进行验证——受支持的假设进入答案支持状态，无法验证的进入隔离区，矛盾的被丢弃。同时使用充分性驱动的停止机制避免不必要的优化。

Result: 在NarraCrime和多个公开推理基准上的实验表明，EVAR在任务性能和证据忠实性两方面均取得提升，同时保持可控的推理成本。

Conclusion: EVAR通过证据验证的假设准入机制有效解决了LLM在长叙事推理中无依据中间假设污染推理轨迹的问题，兼顾了推理质量与成本控制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce fluent but weakly grounded conclusions when reasoning over non-interactive, long-form narratives. A central failure mode is that unsupported intermediate hypotheses can enter the reasoning trajectory and contaminate subsequent inference, especially when evidence is scattered across distant parts of the story. To address this problem, we propose EVAR, an evidence-validated hypothesis admission framework for budget-aware narrative reasoning. EVAR first compiles the narrative into an immutable evidence store of source-linked atomic claims and assigns an instance-specific inference budget from unresolved gaps and uncertainty signals. During refinement, EVAR directly proposes candidate hypotheses for unresolved gaps, constructs hypothesis-conditioned validation challenges, and verifies each candidate against the locked store before admission: supported hypotheses enter the answer-supporting state, unverifiable ones are quarantined, and contradictory ones are discarded. A sufficiency-based stopping mechanism further avoids unnecessary refinement. Experiments on NarraCrime and multiple public reasoning benchmarks show that EVAR improves both task performance and evidence faithfulness while maintaining controllable inference cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [DS-Lighting: Making Agent Harnesses Explicit for Data-Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28590)
*Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DS-Lighting是一个统一的数据科学自动化代理框架工具包，将代理框架（harness）显式分解为数据、工作流、执行和评估四个可复用层，并将多个开源基准统一为MLE-Bench风格的任务格式，以提高端到端数据科学工作流的可复现性、可比性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据科学代理的端到端性能严重依赖于代理框架（harness），但大多数现有系统将框架设计隐式化，导致跨异构任务的结果难以复现、比较和归因。作者旨在使框架设计显式化，以解决这一可复现性和可比性问题。

Method: DS-Lighting将代理框架分解为四个可复用层：数据层、工作流层、执行层和评估层。它通过可执行算子程序表示多样化的代理，同时支持预定义管道和自适应搜索。此外，将多个开源数据科学基准集成为MLE-Bench风格的任务格式，提供共享的任务接口、沙箱运行时和指标协议。

Result: 在代理、框架、模型和消融实验上的实验表明，显式的框架设计提高了可复现性、可比性和可靠性，同时减少了端到端数据科学工作流中可避免的系统级故障。

Conclusion: 显式框架设计是提升数据科学自动化代理系统性能的关键因素，DS-Lighting通过统一、可复用的框架工具包为数据科学自动化提供了更可靠、可复现和可比较的基础设施。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise for automating data-science workflows, yet their end-to-end performance depends critically on the agent harness that represents tasks, manages execution state, constrains output artifacts, and provides evaluation feedback. Existing data-science agents often leave this harness implicit, making results difficult to reproduce, compare, and attribute across heterogeneous tasks. We introduce DS-Lighting, a unified harness toolkit that makes harness design explicit for data-science automation. DS-Lighting decomposes the harness into four reusable layers: data, workflow, execution, and evaluation, and represents diverse agents as executable operator programs that support both predefined pipelines and adaptive search. We further integrate multiple open-source data-science benchmarks into an MLE-Bench-style task format, enabling controlled comparison under a shared task interface, sandboxed runtime, and metric protocol. Experiments across agents, harnesses, models, and ablations show that explicit harness design improves reproducibility, comparability, and reliability, while reducing avoidable system-level failures in end-to-end data-science workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/dslighting

</details>


### [194] [Expert-validated STEM QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28591)
*Kihwan Han,Saurabh Patil,Chinmayee Shukla,Abhinav Sharma,Marko Pavlovic,Anshuman Lall,Mahesh Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"Expert-validated STEM QA"，一个由241位领域专家创建的包含398个问题的STEM（物理、化学、生物学、数学）高质量基准数据集。该数据集针对现有STEM数据集存在的模型性能饱和、分类分布偏斜、多选题格式不匹配真实需求以及答案不准确等问题，通过精心设计的分类体系、质量驱动的贡献者筛选、多轮专家共识修订和可验证的问答应答格式来构建。实验表明前沿AI模型在该数据集上表现低于25%，基于私有版本（N=2,000）的微调使开源模型性能相对基线提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿AI模型消耗了STEM领域大部分可用在线数据，人类专家创建的高质量数据集变得至关重要。然而现有STEM数据集存在四大缺陷：模型性能饱和（无区分度）、分类分布不均衡、多选题格式与科学家实际使用AI方式不匹配、以及基于竞赛收集和时间受限评审导致的答案与推理不准确。这些问题限制了数据集对AI发展的有效推动，亟需一个克服上述缺陷的高质量专家验证数据集。

Method: 作者通过以下方法构建数据集：(1) 精心设计分类体系确保分布均衡；(2) 以质量驱动的激励措施审查题目贡献者；(3) 进行多轮评审，由领域专家基于共识对修订进行验证；(4) 采用可验证的问答应答格式。数据集涵盖物理、化学、生物学和数学四个学科，由241位领域专家创建，共398个问题（同时构建了N=2,000的私有训练版本）。

Result: 在前沿AI模型上测试，该数据集作为基准表现出低于25%的通过率（说明数据集难度较高，模型有足够的提升空间）。使用私营版本（N=2,000）进行后训练微调后，开源模型在HLE-verified数据集的STEM子集上性能相对基线提升15%（p=0.045），具有统计显著性，表明该数据集对模型训练具有实际价值。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个克服现有STEM数据集缺陷的专家验证高质量数据集，既可作为挑战性基准（前沿模型准确率<25%），又可用于模型训练微调来提升性能。部分数据已开源供研究社区使用，为STEM领域AI评估和训练提供了有价值的资源。

Abstract: Recent advancements in AI are helping scientists achieve breakthroughs in fields such as mathematics, medicine, and materials sciences. New evaluation datasets for AI models contribute to such advancement in AI. In the STEM domain, frontier models have consumed most of the available online data, creating the need for human-created datasets that codify the knowledge of leading experts in the domain. There are several STEM datasets available for the research community in this field. However, there are some gaps in these datasets, leaving room for improvement. Examples of gaps include (1) saturation in model performance on these datasets, leaving no head-room for meaningful evaluations, (2) skewed taxonomy distributions, (3) multiple choice question format that is misaligned with how scientists use AI in the real world, and (4) inaccurate answers and rationales partially led by a contest-based data collection and a time-bound review process. In this study, we present 'Expert-validated STEM QA', a high-quality, expert-validated STEM dataset (N=398) in Physics, Chemistry, Biology, and Mathematics, created by 241 domain experts. We (1) carefully designed a taxonomy with balanced distribution, (2) vetted question contributors with quality-driven incentive, (3) conducted multiple rounds of reviews with revisions validated by domain experts based on consensus, and (4) created the dataset in verifiable question and answer format. Our study demonstrated low performance ($<25\%$) of frontier AI models on the dataset as a benchmark. Post-training on a separate, private version of the dataset (N=2,000) increased performance of the open source model by $15\%$ relative to the baseline model (p=0.045) on the STEM subset of HLE-verified dataset, indicating potential utility of the dataset for model training. We have open-sourced a portion of our dataset for the AI research community.

</details>


### [195] [A collective capability boundary in frontier large language models on guideline-conformant and case-specific oncology decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28592)
*Zhang Sheng,Jinming Li,Wangyang Chen,Zhiwei Bao,Yu YoSean Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了肿瘤学决策边界基准（ODBB），包含2,005个肿瘤学决策点，评估了九个前沿LLM（2025年6月至2026年4月发布）。研究发现42.1%的项目没有任何模型能正确回答，模型间失败高度重叠（组合模型无法修复）；追求"果断性"的模型（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro）做出不安全承诺的频率是谨慎模型的3-5倍且得分并未更高；3-9%的项目中模型知道正确步骤却未执行。结论是模型质量不再是临床LLM部署的主要瓶颈，真正的约束是"单一模型作为临床决策唯一基础"的假设，需要架构层面的干预来检测模型能力边界并转交临床医生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基准主要衡量事实记忆，但真实肿瘤学不是知识测试，而是指南路径选择、升级判断和在不确定性下做出承诺的序列决策过程。作者旨在揭示前沿LLM是否存在无法通过模型组合修复的决策路径盲区。

Method: 构建ODBB基准（2,005个决策点，覆盖NCCN指南和结直肠癌病例），使用完全确定性的评分器（零LLM推断）将输出分类为14种失败类型，由两位肿瘤学家独立验证（Cohen加权κ=0.939和0.790，基于225项分层样本）。评估九个前沿LLM（四家闭源、五家开源家族），并将九个模型视为池化超级模型分析失败重叠率。

Result: 42.1%（Wilson 95% CI 40.0-44.3%）的项目没有任何模型正确回答（NCCN项目35.7%，结直肠癌病例66.4%），失败集中在指南路径选择这一"临床元判断"上。GPT-5.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview不安全承诺次数是七个谨慎模型的3-5倍但得分未更高。3-9%的项目中模型正确陈述了下一步但未做出承诺（决策失败而非知识失败）。

Conclusion: 模型质量已不再是临床LLM部署的主要瓶颈；核心约束是任何单一模型都不应作为临床决策的唯一依据。需要开发能够检测模型能力边界并在必要时将决策路由给临床医生的架构。通过更多训练数据无法解决这一盲区，需要架构层面的干预。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve high scores on medical knowledge examinations, yet real-world oncology is not a knowledge test--it is a sequence of guideline-pathway choices, escalation judgments, and commitments under uncertainty. Existing benchmarks largely measure factual recall, leaving open whether frontier LLMs share decision-path blind spots that combining models cannot fix. We built the Oncology Decision Boundary Benchmark (ODBB)--2,005 oncology decision points across NCCN guidelines and colorectal cancer cases--and evaluated nine frontier LLMs (four closed-source, five open-weight families) released between June 2025 and April 2026. A fully deterministic scorer (zero LLM inference) classified outputs into 14 failure types, independently validated by two oncologists (Cohen's weighted $κ$ = 0.939 and 0.790) on a 225-item stratified sample. Treating the nine as a pooled super-model, 42.1% (Wilson 95% CI 40.0--44.3%) of all items--35.7% of the 1,586 NCCN items and 66.4% of the 419 colorectal-cancer cases--were answered correctly by none, with failures concentrated in choosing between guideline pathways before reasoning within any: a consistent blind spot in clinical meta-judgment that likely requires architectural intervention rather than more training data. Two models tuned for decisiveness (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview) made unsafe commitments three to five times more often than the seven cautious models without scoring higher. In 3--9% of items, models stated the correct next clinical step yet did not commit to it--failures of decision, not knowledge. Model quality is no longer the primary bottleneck for clinical LLM deployment; the binding constraint is the assumption that any single model can be the sole basis for a clinical decision. Progress requires architectures that detect when a model reaches its competence boundary and route the decision to a clinician.

</details>


### [196] [Statutory AI: Aligning Large Language Models With Legal Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28593)
*Cindy Delage,Stéphane Canu,Marc Décombas,Jonathan Foureur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Statutory AI——一种混合式AI对齐方法，利用法律语料库中的人类撰写的原则作为宪法框架，使AI系统能自主评审和修正输出。该方法采用两阶段Chain-of-Thought推理：先对用户提示分类到特定法律主题，再结合相关法律条款进行分析。实验表明该方法在减少有害内容方面比Constitutional AI高约10个百分点，同时计算时间减少50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法存在局限：Constitutional AI依赖人类监督，而Good-for-Humanity等广泛规范框架过于笼统模糊，难以提供可操作的治理指导。随着AI监管框架的发展，需要更具体、可操作的对齐方法确保生成模型符合法律和伦理标准。

Method: 提出Statutory AI混合方法，利用法律语料库中特定主题的人类撰写的原则作为宪法框架。采用两阶段操作，均使用Chain-of-Thought提示：第一阶段将用户提示分类到已识别的主题中，第二阶段将提示与从该主题法律语料库中选取的相关条款结合分析。实验使用1,000个红队攻击提示和五个刑罚主题（歧视、泄露机密信息、暴力、欺诈、虐待弱势群体）进行测试。

Result: Statutory AI在多个测试模型上将有害内容减少了52到59个百分点，比标准Constitutional AI高出约10个百分点，同时计算时间降低超过50%。

Conclusion: Statutory AI通过利用现有的法律文本作为对齐框架，克服了Constitutional AI对人工监督的依赖和广泛规范框架表述含糊的局限，在有效性和计算效率方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: With the increasing development of AI regulatory frameworks, ensuring that artificial intelligence systems, particularly generative models, operate in accordance with legal and ethical standards has become a critical priority. Existing proposals for AI alignment and value-guided behavior, however, face some limitations. Approaches such as Constitutional AI depend on human supervision, while broad normative frameworks like the Good-for-Humanity (GfH) principle may be overly general and ambiguous to provide actionable governance guidance. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid approach called Statutory AI that employs pre-existing human-authored principles drawn from specific themes within a legal corpus. Specifically, Statutory AI uses legal texts as a constitutional framework, enabling AI systems to autonomously critique and revise their outputs according to established norms. It operates in two stages, both using Chain-of-Thought prompting. The first stage classifies the user prompt into one of the identified themes, while the second stage analyzes it in conjunction with relevant articles selected from the legal corpus of that theme. To illustrate the potential of our approach, we conducted an experiment involving 1,000 red-teaming prompts and five penal themes: discrimination, disclosure of confidential information, violence, fraud, and abuse of vulnerable persons. Statutory AI reduced harmful content by 52 to 59 percentage points across tested models, approximately 10 percentage points higher than standard Constitutional AI, while cutting computation time by over 50%.

</details>


### [197] [From Question-First to Analyst-First: Domain-Expert Skills and Verified Knowledge Compilation for Proactive Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28594)
*Harmohit Singh,Rahul Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"分析师优先"（analyst-first）的生产级对话式分析系统，通过两个核心架构思想——可插拔领域专家"技能"抽象层和离线知识编译循环——将交互模式从"先提问题"反转为"先出结果"，使缺乏经验或查询日志的非专家用户无需编写查询即可获得验证过的数据洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式分析系统假设用户已能提出结构良好的问题，但非专家面对陌生企业模式和空白查询框时无从下手。商业"主动式"工具仅能检测分析师既定指标层上的统计异常，学术界的下一步问题推荐器又依赖查询日志，而新数据集没有此类日志。亟需一种不依赖用户提问能力和历史日志的主动分析架构。

Method: 两个耦合的架构思想：(1) 可插拔领域专家"技能"抽象——一种基于文件夹、无数据库的领域知识包（含manifest、分阶段提示面、关键字路由引用、报告模板和可选计算），通过确定性schema匹配按(客户,数据集)自动选择，作为横切关注点织入agent流水线、schema探索器和报告引擎的各阶段，缺失时降级为严格no-op；技能是自包含文件夹且确定性解析，故目录可无限扩展为领域专家市场。(2) 离线知识编译循环——agent通过DuckDB探测parquet数据（零生产负载），运行带有批评门控的逐表收敛和自愈重试，并通过值重叠校验join，产生持久schema知识，驱动常驻专家报告；每个发布的指标通过重新执行其证据SQL验证，同时生成镜像报告议程的建议问题，形成"报告出数字→数字生问题→点击进入验证深潜"的主动闭环。

Result: 给出了形式化模型，并提供了说明性的单租户实证证据。作者明确不作用户研究或基准测试声明，强调贡献在于架构本身及其可辩护性（defensibility）。

Conclusion: 该系统的核心贡献是一种可防御的架构：通过领域技能的市场化可扩展性与离线知识编译的自动化，构建了一个无需用户先提问、无需查询日志的主动式分析系统。作者有意将工作定位为架构贡献而非实证贡献，留给后续研究验证其端到端效果。

Abstract: Conversational analytics systems assume the user already has a well-formed question, leaving a non-expert facing a blank query box on an unfamiliar enterprise schema. Commercial 'proactive' tools narrow this gap only by detecting statistical anomalies over analyst-curated metric layers, and academic next-question recommenders depend on query logs that a fresh dataset lacks. We describe a production analytics system that inverts the interaction model from question-first to analyst-first through two coupled architectural ideas. First, a pluggable domain-expert 'skill' abstraction: a folder-based, database-free subject-matter pack (a manifest, per-stage prompt facets, keyword-routed references, report templates, and optional compute) auto-selected per (client, dataset) by deterministic schema matching and spliced as a cross-cutting concern into every stage of an agentic pipeline, the schema explorer, and the report engines, degrading to a strict no-op when absent. Because a skill is a self-contained folder resolved deterministically, the catalogue is open-ended: an extensible marketplace of domain experts. Second, an offline knowledge-compilation loop: an agent probes the dataset's parquet via DuckDB (zero load on production), runs critic-gated per-table convergence with self-healing retries, and data-validates joins by value overlap, producing durable schema knowledge that drives standing expert reports whose every published metric is re-verified by re-executing its evidence SQL, plus suggested questions that mirror the report agenda. These close a proactive loop: reports surface numbers, the numbers seed questions, and a click launches a verified deep dive, all before the query box is used. We give a formal model and report illustrative single-tenant evidence. We make no user-study or benchmark claims; the contribution is the architecture and its defensibility.

</details>


### [198] [The Signal in the Noise: An Auditable Reliability Layer for Biomedical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28595)
*Moustafa Yehia Hassan,Sharon Wong,Woh Kai Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向生物医学NLP管道的保守、可审计的拼写修正可靠性层，作为安全性优先的预处理模块，在不确定时放弃编辑（医疗"不伤害"原则），通过有界编辑距离候选生成、语料库n-gram评分和领域安全门控，在CORD-19三分类任务上恢复约80.45%的噪声导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学NLP流水线通常假设输入文本干净，但大规模语料库经自动PDF解析后存在大量OCR类伪影、词元分裂合并、连字符残留和字符级损坏，系统性侵蚀词汇证据并降低下游分类器性能。现有校正方法追求最大精确度，缺乏对领域关键术语的保护和可审计性。

Method: 构建确定性、保守的拼写修正可靠性层：(1) 有界编辑距离候选生成；(2) 语料库衍生的n-gram评分；(3) 生物医学安全门控保护领域关键术语。系统在不确定条件下不进行编辑，遵循医疗"不伤害"原则。评测采用内在（2,104条人工标注token级基准）和外在（CORD-19三分类，10,000样例，四轮协议：Clean/Noisy/Restored/Safety）两种方式，另附103篇真实OCR摘要的BioBERT案例研究。

Result: 内在评测：错误修复召回率94.61%，负面对照零有害编辑。下游评测：恢复约80.45%的噪声诱导宏F1退化，宏F1从0.7654（Noisy）提升至0.7717（Restored），Safety模式下达0.7721。案例研究表明transformer编码器对轻度噪声相对鲁棒，从而启发未来灰盒架构。

Conclusion: 系统完全确定性、伪影驱动且面向部署与审计设计，验证了"保守比激进安全"的预处理理念在生物医学NLP中的价值；未来方向是融合有界神经信号与UMLS词表的灰盒架构，在不牺牲可审计性的前提下进一步提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Biomedical NLP pipelines routinely presuppose clean input text, yet large-scale corpora assembled through automated PDF parsing harbour pervasive OCR-like artifacts, token splits and merges, hyphenation remnants, and character-level corruption, that systematically erode lexical evidence and degrade downstream classifiers. We introduce a conservative, fully auditable spell-correction reliability layer conceived as a safety-oriented preprocessing module rather than a maximal-accuracy corrector: under conditions of uncertainty, the system abstains from editing, in accordance with a medical do-no-harm philosophy. The deterministic architecture couples bounded edit-distance candidate generation with corpus-derived n-gram scoring and a suite of biomedical safety gates that protect domain-critical terminology. We evaluate the layer both intrinsically, on a manually curated benchmark of 2,104 token-level cases, and extrinsically, on a tri-class CORD-19 topic classifier (Prevention, Treatment, Epidemiology) spanning 10,000 examples under a principled four-run protocol (Clean, Noisy, Restored, Safety). Intrinsically, the layer attains 94.61% error-fix recall on synthetic errors with zero harmful edits on negative controls. Downstream, it recovers approximately 80.45% of the noise-induced macro-F1 degradation, elevating macro-F1 from 0.7654 (Noisy) to 0.7717 (Restored) while preserving near-clean performance (Safety: 0.7721). A supplementary case study on 103 real-world OCR-extracted abstracts classified with BioBERT confirms that transformer encoders appeared relatively robust to mild noise, motivating a future grey-box architecture that integrates bounded neural signals and UMLS lexicons without compromising auditability. The system is fully deterministic, artifact-driven, and designed with deployment and auditability in mind.

</details>


### [199] [Paper Pilot: A Human-in-the-Loop Expert System for Evidence-Traceable Scientific Manuscript Generation in Applied Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28596)
*Nidhi Jha,Siddharth Chaudhary,Ajinkya Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Paper Pilot——一个面向应用科学的、人在环路的证据可追溯科学手稿生成专家系统，通过八个人工审批门、证据锁定修订控制和声明分类机制，将LLM辅助写作定位为受控的人类-AI决策支持流程，而非全自主写作管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统虽然推进了自主发现和手稿生成，但未能解决AI辅助工作流中想法、方法、结果和声明传播时缺乏强制性人工审批和人工制品级可追溯性的治理问题。

Method: 将协作式智能体推理工程（CARE）方法适应到手稿开发中，引入了手稿所有者审批门、明确的不过关标准、声明分类、审计日志、顾问式LLM评审和证据锁定修订控制。定义了从想法到声明的管线中的八个人工审批门，区分文献支撑声明与人工制品支撑声明，并要求报告的数字和解释可追溯到已批准的证据。系统提示词开源发布。

Result: 在引用接地层的实证验证中（两个商业LLM、真实arXiv论文、无LLM评判者的受控机械评分基准）：在覆盖压力下，无门的起草器制造了高达25%的虚假引用且从未标记证据缺口；而采用Paper Pilot证据锁定规则后，零虚假引用产生，并将植入的缺口以显式占位符形式浮现。结果接地、修订和对抗鲁棒性的初步结果方向相同。

Conclusion: Paper Pilot将LLM辅助写作定位为受控的人类-AI决策支持过程，而非完全自主的作者身份管线，有效解决了AI辅助科研写作中的可追溯性和治理问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly embedded in scientific workflows for literature analysis, drafting, and review. Existing systems advance autonomous discovery and manuscript generation, but do not resolve the governance problem that arises when ideas, methods, results, and claims propagate through AI-assisted workflows without mandatory human approval or artifact-level traceability. This paper proposes Paper Pilot, a human-in-the-loop expert system for evidence-traceable scientific manuscript generation in applied sciences. It adapts the Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE) methodology to manuscript development through manuscript-owner approval gates, explicit no-pass criteria, claim classification, audit logging, advisory LLM review, and evidence-locked revision control. The framework defines eight approval gates across the idea-to-claim pipeline and distinguishes literature-grounded from artifact-grounded claims, requiring reported numbers and interpretations to remain traceable to approved evidence; its system prompt is openly released for deployment in ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, or institutional LLM environments. As a first empirical validation, we evaluate the citation-grounding layer with a controlled, mechanically scored benchmark (two commercial LLMs, real arXiv papers, no LLM judge): under coverage pressure ungated drafters fabricated up to 25% of their citations and never flagged an evidence gap, whereas the same models under Paper Pilot's evidence-locked rules produced zero fabricated citations and surfaced the planted gaps as explicit placeholders. Preliminary results for result grounding, revision, and adversarial robustness point the same way; full evaluation is left to future work. Paper Pilot positions LLM-assisted writing as a controlled human-AI decision-support process rather than a fully autonomous authorship pipeline.

</details>


### [200] [The Race between Agentic AI Capabilities and Data Quality Control in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28597)
*Sourav Panda,Hillmer Chona,Rupak Kumar Das,Shreyash Kale,Shikha Soneji,Jonathan Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于LLM的智能体AI在完成在线问卷和注意力检查方面的能力，从攻击（利用DOM元数据和选项编码等结构性漏洞）和防御（DOM元数据混淆）两个角度进行了实验评估，并提出了兼顾实证研究者和智能体AI研究者需求的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线问卷是各领域的基础数据收集工具，注意力检查是保障回答质量的关键机制。随着具备工具增强能力和多模态处理的智能体AI的快速出现，这些保障措施的鲁棒性受到质疑，需要系统评估智能体AI对问卷完成和注意力检查的渗透能力。

Method: 在受控的问卷沙盒环境中，评估一个支持多模态输入处理和基于工具网页交互的单智能体架构；从攻击视角展示DOM元数据暴露和可预测选项编码等结构漏洞使智能体可通过结构化解析通过注意力检查；从防御视角实施DOM元数据混淆策略以移除文本问题中的语义线索；评估多种开源语言和多模态模型以研究能力和编排有效性。

Result: 攻击视角下，结构性漏洞（如DOM元数据暴露、选项编码可预测）足以让智能体仅通过结构化解析就绕过注意力检查；防御视角下，DOM元数据混淆能有效移除语义线索，降低被利用风险；不同开源模型在能力与编排规划上表现各异。

Conclusion: 智能体AI能够利用问卷系统的结构性漏洞通过注意力检查，但DOM元数据混淆等防御策略可以一定程度上缓解风险。研究为实证研究者如何设计更鲁棒的问卷系统，以及智能体AI研究者如何理解agent系统交互能力提供了双向的见解和建议。

Abstract: Online surveys are a foundational data collection instrument in a variety of fields, with attention checks serving as critical guardians of response quality. However, the rapid emergence of agentic AI (goal directed systems powered by a large language model (LLM) brain and/or a multimodal processing unit with tool-augmented capabilities) raises new questions about the robustness of these safeguards. We investigate how well agentic AI architectures can complete web-based surveys and pass standard attention checks. We evaluate a single-agent architecture capable of multimodal input processing and tool-based web interaction on a controlled survey sandbox. We analyze the problem from two perspectives. From an attack perspective, we demonstrate how structural vulnerabilities such as exposed DOM metadata and predictable option encoding allow agents to resolve attention checks through structured parsing only. From a defense perspective, we implement a mitigation strategy of DOM metadata obfuscation to remove semantic cues in text-based questions. We evaluate multiple open-source language and multimodal models to study capability and orchestration effectiveness. Based on our evaluations, we offer perspectives on how to simultaneously meet the needs of empiricists and agentic AI researchers.

</details>


### [201] [CDPR: Counterfactual Advantage-based Credit Assignment for Cost-Aware Sequential Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28599)
*Qi Peng,Yi Cai,Changmeng Zheng,Xin Wu,Jiayuan Xie,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CDPR（反事实诊断过程奖励）方法，将临床诊断建模为成本感知的顺序决策过程，通过强化学习训练策略。CDPR无需专家标签和学习评论家，利用动作分布不确定性定位策略犹豫状态，并通过短回滚估计所选动作相对替代方案的优势来评分，集成到GRPO中在多个基准上提升了诊断准确性同时减少了检查数量和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学语言模型将诊断视为一次性分类任务，忽略了真实临床诊断中逐步检查、观察结果、更新诊断的序贯过程以及检查价值与成本之间的权衡。判断奖励存在的主要难点是信用分配问题——可靠的信号只在长轨迹结束时出现一次，导致浪费型检查方案与高效方案得分相同，无法有效训练策略。

Method: 将诊断建模为成本感知的序贯决策过程，用强化学习训练策略。提出CDPR方法：首先利用策略动作分布的确定性来识别策略犹豫的状态；然后在这些状态下，通过短回滚在平衡正确性、检查数量、成本和不可行请求的效用函数下，估计所选动作相对于策略自身会考虑的替代方案的advantage来评分。引入回滚缓存重用批内轨迹以降低计算成本，并将CDPR集成到GRPO中。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV（域内）以及ClinicalBench和私有医院数据集（两个域外）基准上测试。CDPR在提高诊断准确性的同时，显著减少了检查数量和成本。

Conclusion: CDPR作为无需专家标签和学习评论家的奖励设计方法，能够有效解决成本感知序贯诊断决策中的信用分配问题，在提升诊断正确性的同时降低检查数量和费用，展现了对域外数据的泛化能力，为医学语言模型应用于真实临床诊断流程提供了可行的训练范式。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is a step-by-step, cost-aware process: a physician orders examinations one at a time, observes the results, and updates the diagnosis before reaching a final conclusion. Most medical language models instead treat diagnosis as a one-pass classification task and ignore the trade-off between a test's value and its cost. We model diagnosis as a cost-aware sequential decision process and train the policy with reinforcement learning. The main difficulty is credit assignment: the only reliable signal comes once at the end of a long trajectory, so it scores a wasteful workup the same as an efficient one. We propose CDPR (Counterfactual Diagnostic Process Reward), which needs no expert labels and no learned critic. CDPR first finds the states where the policy hesitates, using the uncertainty of its action distribution, and then scores the chosen action by its advantage over the alternatives the policy itself would consider, estimated with short rollouts under a utility that balances correctness against test count, cost, and infeasible requests. A rollout cache reuses within-batch trajectories to keep the cost low. We integrate CDPR into GRPO and test it on one in-domain (MIMIC-IV) and two out-of-domain (ClinicalBench and a private hospital dataset) benchmarks. CDPR improves diagnostic accuracy while clearly reducing the number and cost of examinations.

</details>


### [202] [SHAPE of Chain-of-Thought in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28600)
*Jonghyun Song,Sangjun Song,Minjae Oh,Haesung Pyun,Sungsik Lee,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHAPE框架，从数学教育中的两个维度（语义空间和启发式）分析大语言模型的思维链推理过程，发现启发式比传统CoT特征更能解释答案正确性，模型偏向于在少数语义空间中集中推理努力，并基于此开发了促进启发式多样性的后训练方法以提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理基准上表现强劲，但其推理背后所依赖的数学意义技能（如语义解释和启发式使用）仍未被充分探索，缺乏一个理论上严谨的框架来系统诊断CoT推理模式。

Method: 提出SHAPE框架，从数学教育中的两个透镜分析CoT轨迹：（1）语义空间——模型对问题不断演化的数学解释（如代数、几何）；（2）启发式——在这些空间内采取的具体数学操作（如简化问题、逆向推导）。框架被用于分析推理模式、评估后训练效果，并指导启发式多样化的后训练。

Result: （1）模型使用的数学启发式比传统CoT特征更能解释最终答案的正确性；（2）模型更可能通过在少数语义空间内集中推理努力而非探索多个不相关空间来达到正确答案，这与人类行为一致；（3）强化学习后训练会在启发式使用上诱导模式化（mode-seeking）行为；（4）通过促进多样化的启发式进行后训练，可有效提升模型准确率。

Conclusion: SHAPE提供了一个具有理论基础的诊断框架来解码LLM推理，并为通过多样化启发式后训练增强LLM数学推理开辟了新的路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yet the mathematically meaningful skills underlying their reasoning remain underexplored. We introduce \texttt{SHAPE}, a framework that analyzes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories through two lenses developed in mathematics education: (1) semantic spaces: the model's evolving mathematical interpretations of a problem (e.g., algebraic, geometric), and (2) heuristics: the specific mathematical actions taken within those spaces (e.g., simplifying the problem, working backward). We first use \texttt{SHAPE} to analyze the reasoning patterns of various models. Our findings reveal that the mathematical heuristics employed by a model better explain final answer correctness than traditional CoT features. Furthermore, models are likely to reach correct solutions by concentrating their reasoning effort within a few semantic spaces rather than exploring many disparate ones -- a pattern consistent with human behavior. Next, we utilize the \texttt{SHAPE} lens to evaluate whether post-training truly enhances mathematical proficiency. We find that reinforcement learning induces mode-seeking in heuristic usage. Lastly, we post-train LLMs by promoting diverse heuristics and demonstrate its effectiveness in improving accuracy. Overall, \texttt{SHAPE} provides a theoretically-grounded diagnostic framework for decoding LLM reasoning and offers a new path toward post-training LLMs for math reasoning. The code for our model is available at https://github.com/holi-lab/SHAPE-of-CoT

</details>


### [203] [Leveraging Generative AI to Design Accessible Interactive Visualizations for Undergraduate Mathematics: A Six-Phase Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28601)
*Mahesh Sunkula,Kuan-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个六阶段工作流，利用生成式AI帮助无编程经验的教师构建符合WCAG 2.2 AA标准的数学交互式可视化工具。研究通过四个部署工具验证了该工作流的可行性，但强调每个阶段的输出仍需人工验证数学正确性、可访问性和教学契合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式可视化能促进本科数学的概念理解，但构建这类工具通常需要编程技能，而大多数数学教师不具备这种能力。研究者希望通过生成式AI降低门槛，使教师无需编程即可构建合规的可视化工具。

Method: 采用基于设计的研究方法，开发了包含六个阶段的工作流（基础、定制、数学深度、应用、可访问性、教学控制），用于指导生成式AI构建可视化工具。每个阶段结构化提示词，辅助AI代码生成，并定义人工验证介入点。研究同时测试了工作流正向（从自然语言想法生成工具）和逆向（从成品工具反向推导提示词）的可行性。

Result: 研究部署了四个覆盖微积分、多元微积分和微分方程的工具，通过闭式解评估数学正确性、自动化和人工屏幕阅读器测试评估可访问性。结果表明该工作流能生成结构完整的一遍通过工具，但每个阶段都必须进行人工验证，包括数学正确性、可访问性和教学适配性检查。

Conclusion: 六阶段工作流是平台无关的，适用于教师和学生双方，能有效利用生成式AI构建数学可视化工具。然而AI输出尚不能完全替代人工审查，在每个阶段保留人工验证是不可或缺的环节。

Abstract: Interactive visualizations support conceptual understanding in undergraduate mathematics, but building them has required programming expertise most instructors lack.
