Result: 在固定生产衍生开发基准和公共GeoNames迁移任务上，任务适配相比冻结编码器和标准token基线有显著提升。GeoNames上适配模型在零至中等字符重叠范围内提升已知目标Recall@1；盲人对比实验中相关P@1从28.0%提升至46.0%（p=0.012）；固定查询端点估计在非规范查询上有改善，随机在线实验未检测到参与度回归。

Conclusion: 任务适配的地理实体检索可以实用地替代现有的基于词法分类的标准化器，在非规范查询上获得最大相关性增益。

Abstract: People search must map free-form location phrases to geographic entities used as structured retrieval filters. Lexical standardizers handle canonical names well but are brittle to aliases, misspellings, metropolitan expressions, and same-name ambiguity. We formulate this task as graded, set-valued entity retrieval over a fixed ontology. We identify three coupled design requirements: distinguishing identity-preserving variation from knowledge-dependent aliases, controlling false negatives among valid same-name entities, and separating stable transformations from mutable entity knowledge. We realize them in a prompt-asymmetric bi-encoder with calibrated alias support, bounded ambiguity-aware negatives, and editable entity documents that support localized updates without retraining.
  Across a fixed production-derived development benchmark and a public GeoNames transfer task, task adaptation improves substantially over frozen encoders and standard token baselines. Controlled development ablations show that specialized supervision contributes beyond standard task fine-tuning and encoder scaling. On GeoNames, the adapted model improves known-target Recall@1 throughout zero-to-moderate character overlap, while character n-grams retain a small aggregate Target Recall@5 advantage. In a blinded human comparison on a stratified production challenge set, our model raises relevant P@1 from 28.0% to 46.0% (p=0.012). Fixed-query endpoint estimates improve on non-canonical queries and remain close to control on frequent queries; a randomized live experiment detects no engagement regression. These results support task-adapted geographic entity retrieval as a practical replacement for the incumbent taxonomy-based standardizer, with the largest relevance gains on non-canonical queries.

</details>


### [238] [Efficient GPU Retrieval for Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28968)
*Dhritiman Das,Chujie Zheng,Ronak Kaoshik,Pratik Dixit,Vishal Shah,Yanbo Li,Jiahao Xu,Manika Agarwal,Chinmay Naik,Lingyu Zhang,Chetan Bhole,Chirag Bhanuprasad Mehta,Meng Zheng,Puneet Singh Ahluwalia,Shirisha Singh,Ping Jin,Manas Apte,Gokulraj Mohanasundaram,Tugrul Bingol,Raghavan Muthuregunathan,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinkedIn提出了一种策略对齐的语义检索框架：将嵌入划分为八个类别监督的段落，在服务时遵循与非协商因素一致的min/median聚合规则，结合单槽Stage-1打分器和尺度不变的相对归一化门控，配合FP8粗排+FP16精排的两阶段GPU架构，使探索性查询Precision@10从63.7%提升至79.0%，导航性查询Precision@1从65.5%提升至74.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn的语义搜索需要从数亿人档案中检索匹配自然语言查询的相关画像。现有的LLM分级相关性（GR）判官采用固定min/median聚合规则，逐条强制满足所有不可协商的方面；而余弦相似度是平均证据，允许一个方面的强匹配掩盖另一方面的失败，这限制了第一阶段（L0）检索器的召回能力。因此需要一种与部署策略对齐的检索框架。

Method: 1) 将嵌入划分为八个类别监督的段落，每个段落的得分在服务时遵循与GR判官相同的min/median规则；多向量检索时，每段得分按标记槽位独立计算并在槽位间取最大值。2) 使用轻量级单槽Stage-1打分器生成高召回候选。3) 引入尺度不变的相对归一化门控，使类别激活在训练、评估和服务阶段保持一致。4) 服务端采用两阶段GPU架构：FP8粗排器对全量语料打分（每分片容量提升71%，Stage-1矩阵乘法吞吐提升36%），随后FP16精确重排过采样候选集，在每分片副本500+ QPS下恢复99.6-99.8%的全FP16召回率。

Result: 在21K留出查询上，该表示方法相比匹配容量的基线在离线相关性上取得提升，收益广泛分布于各因素组合中。A/B测试中，不变的GR判官下探索性查询Precision@10从63.7%提升到79.0%，导航性查询Precision@1从65.5%提升到74.7%；盲审人类评估独立确认了Precision@10的提升。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将检索表征设计为与部署相关性策略（而非简单余弦相似度）对齐，能够显著提升语义检索质量；两阶段FP8/FP16混合精度GPU架构在保持高吞吐的同时几乎不损失精度，验证了该框架在实际工业规模系统的可行性。

Abstract: Semantic Search on LinkedIn must retrieve relevant profiles from a corpus of hundreds of millions in response to natural-language queries such as "a fintech founder in Berlin who worked in payments." The deployed relevance policy is bottleneck-oriented: every active non-negotiable facet must be satisfied, and a pre-existing LLM Graded Relevance (GR) judge operationalizes this through a fixed min/median aggregation over facet grades. Cosine similarity instead averages evidence, letting a strong match on one facet mask failure on another, capping the recall of the first-stage (L0) retriever.
  We present a policy-aligned retrieval framework: embeddings are partitioned into eight category-supervised segments whose scores follow the same min/median rule at serving time; for multi-vector retrieval, this segment score is computed independently per tagged document slot and maximized across slots. A lightweight single-slot Stage-1 scorer generates high-recall candidates, while scale-invariant relative-norm gating keeps category activation consistent across training, evaluation, and serving. On 21K held-out queries, this representation improves offline relevance over a matched-capacity baseline, with gains broadly distributed across facet combinations.
  We serve this framework with a two-stage GPU architecture: an FP8 coarse ranker scores the full corpus, increasing per-shard capacity by 71% and Stage-1 matmul throughput by 36%, then an FP16 stage exactly re-ranks an oversampled candidate set, recovering 99.6-99.8% of full-FP16 recall at over 500 QPS per shard replica. In a member-randomized A/B test, exploratory-query Precision@10 under the unchanged GR judge rises from 63.7% to 79.0% and navigational Precision@1 from 65.5% to 74.7%, with a blinded human evaluation independently confirming the Precision@10 gain.

</details>


### [239] [From Analytics to Tumor Boards: An Evidence-Linked Multi-Agent Workflow for Oncology Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28974)
*Daniel Kang,Michelle Hu,Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Shayan Vassef,Yufan Wang,Anit Kumar Sahu,Munmun De Choudhury,Vedant Das Swain,Christian Poellabauer,Li Yan Khor,Koustuv Saha,Robert Wojciechowski,Elliot Kidd,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），一种可配置的肿瘤学信息抽取工作流，能够从碎片化的肿瘤学文档中提取328个临床相关结构化字段。在40名患者的230份去标识化文档（含418个临床医生审核的文档-字段对、1,126个非空参考值）上，nMAS达到了82.6%的rank-weighted精确率、87.5%的召回率和85.0%的F1分数，显著优于基准UMA-style MiniMax M2.5的66.4% F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床相关的肿瘤学信息分布在异质、纵向的文档中，人工癌症登记抽象平均每例需要27.2分钟，造成巨大的抽象负担且难以保证跨标本、肿瘤、生物标志物和时间点的准确归因。亟需可扩展的方法，在保留临床上下文的同时将碎片化文档转换为结构化数据。

Method: nMAS将临床医生定义的字段规范与模型执行分离，结合了复杂度感知抽取（complexity-aware extraction）、报告级整合（report-level consolidation）和源文档接地验证（source-grounded validation）。评估采用临床医生审核的文档-字段对，聚焦于临床医生确认在源文档中存在的字段，而非穷举标注全部328个模式字段。

Result: nMAS在rank-weighted精确率上达到82.6%，召回率87.5%，F1分数85.0%，显著优于独立实现的UMA-style MiniMax M2.5比较器的66.4% F1分数。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，使用可配置的、源文档接地的抽取工作流将碎片化肿瘤学文档转化为可复用的结构化数据是可行的。

Abstract: Clinically relevant oncology information is distributed across heterogeneous, longitudinal documentation, creating substantial abstraction burden and requiring accurate attribution across specimens, tumors, biomarkers, and time points, while manual cancer-registry abstraction can require 27.2 minutes per case, highlighting the need for scalable methods that preserve clinical context while converting documentation into structured data. We evaluate the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), a configurable oncology information-extraction workflow which extracts clinically relevant structured fields from fragmented oncology documentation. The extraction task uses a clinician-informed schema of 328 attributes spanning report metadata, diagnosis, staging, and cancer-type-specific information. nMAS separates clinician-defined field specifications from model execution and combines complexity-aware extraction, report-level consolidation, and source-grounded validation. The retrospective evaluation included 230 de-identified oncology documents from 40 patients and 418 clinician-reviewed document-field pairs containing 1,126 non-empty reference values. Evaluation focused on fields identified by clinicians as present in the source documents rather than exhaustively annotating all 328 schema fields. nMAS achieved a rank-weighted value-level precision of 82.6%, recall of 87.5%, and F1 of 85.0%, compared with an F1 of 66.4% for an independently implemented UMA-style MiniMax M2.5 comparator. These findings support the feasibility of using a configurable, source-grounded extraction workflow to convert fragmented oncology documentation into reusable structured data.

</details>


### [240] [The Role of Network Topology and Opponent Information in Shaping Cooperation in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28977)
*Seongho Son,Stephen Hailes,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究图拓扑结构对深度强化学习智能体在两人迭代囚徒困境中合作行为的影响，发现邻居数量和平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的关键因素；伙伴选择可促进合作，而提供对手身份信息反而阻碍合作策略的扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注基于策略模仿的图拓扑对合作的影a响，但缺乏对深度强化学习智能体在社交图结构下学习合作行为的研究。

Method: 将每个智能体表示为图中的节点，邻居作为交互对手池；智能体使用深度强化学习玩两人迭代囚徒困境，并在每轮实验中提供不同类型的对手信息（动作历史和对手身份），在不同图拓扑下进行实验对比。

Result: 实验表明，每节点的邻居数和图平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的主要因素；伙伴选择（限制对手多样性）促进互惠合作，而提供对手身份信息会抑制合作策略的传播。

Conclusion: 图结构显著影响深度强化学习智能体在IPD中的合作演化，对手池多样性和身份信息都会影响合作策略的学习与传播。

Abstract: Several works have investigated the influence of graph topology on cooperation among artificial agents, while the majority of the literature has focused on modelling agents' adaptation through strategy imitation, which relies solely on the cumulative payoffs of others. This paper investigates scenarios in which each agent learns to play the two-player Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD) using deep reinforcement learning. Each agent is represented as a node in a graph, where its neighbours constitute the pool of opponents with whom it can interact. During each IPD episode, agents are provided with different types of information about their opponent, consisting of action history and opponent identity. Experimental results across different graph topologies show that the number of neighbours per node and the average path length are the main factors affecting the emergence of cooperation. We also show that, while partner selection fosters mutual cooperation by limiting the diversity of the opponent pool, providing agents with the identity of their opponent hinders the proliferation of cooperative strategies.

</details>


### [241] [Selective Forgetting: A Graph-Based Memory Framework for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28978)
*Theo Rusu,Sourena Khanzadeh,Manar Alalfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文直接评估了知识图谱作为长期智能体记忆的假设：在LongMemEval基准上，基于两跳子图问答的提取式图谱并未超越扁平向量检索基线（token F1 0.417 vs 0.468），尤其在回忆特定助手回合的问题上表现显著更差（0.607 vs 0.911）。但遗忘模块有效：在27,021节点图谱上仅去除9.8%节点，F1几乎不变（+0.001），正确率仅降1.6点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱被广泛提出作为扁平RAG的结构化替代方案用于长期智能体记忆，核心假设是将对话表示为实体和关系能提升回忆能力。然而该假设从未被直接严格检验，本文旨在直面并实证评估这一假设。

Method: 提取式图谱管道：(1) 将每个对话轮次提取为带类型的节点和带属性的边；(2) 从两跳子图中回答查询；(3) 周期性根据加权组合（新近性、访问频率、度中心性、年龄）的分数对节点进行剪枝。采用匹配的候选生成预算（5个检索根）与扁平向量基线对比，使用paired bootstrap统计检验。

Result: 图谱未超越基线：token F1为0.417对0.468，paired bootstrap给出Δ=-0.050（95%CI [-0.085,-0.016]）。在需回忆特定助手历史回合的问题上差距最大（正确率0.911降至0.607），因将轮次分解为实体丢弃了问题的表层形式。遗忘模块在27,021节点图谱上删除9.8%节点和9.5%字节，F1保持不变(+0.001，CI [-0.015,+0.016])，正确率仅降1.6点。

Conclusion: 基于提取的图谱管道在同等检索预算下不优于扁平检索，但遗忘/剪枝模块在几乎不损失性能的前提下显著节省存储。由于仅使用单一小模型提取器且只在一个基准上评估，结果表征的是该提取式管道而非图结构记忆的普适性。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have been proposed as a structured alternative to flat retrieval-augmented generation for long-term agent memory, on the assumption that representing conversations as entities and relations improves recall. We evaluate that assumption directly. Our framework extracts each conversational turn into typed nodes and attributed edges, answers questions from a two-hop subgraph, and periodically prunes nodes that score low on a weighted combination of recency, access frequency, degree centrality, and age. On LongMemEval, the graph does not outperform a flat vector baseline at a matched candidate-generation budget of five retrieval roots: token F1 is $0.417$ against $0.468$, and a paired bootstrap over 500 questions gives
$Δ= -0.050$ (95\% CI $[-0.085, -0.016]$). The gap is widest on questions that require recalling a specific prior assistant turn, where judged correctness falls from $0.911$ to $0.607$, suggesting that decomposing a turn into entities discards the surface form these questions depend on. The forgetting module is more successful. Applied once to a persistent 27{,}021-node graph, it removes 9.8\% of nodes and 9.5\% of stored bytes; token F1 is unchanged ($+0.001$, 95\% CI $[-0.015, +0.016]$) and judged correctness falls by $1.6$ points, with the 95\% interval bounding any loss at $3.8$ points ($[-0.038, +0.006]$). Because our extractor is a single small model evaluated on one benchmark, these results characterise this extraction-based pipeline rather than graph-structured memory in general. Code: https://github.com/skhanzad/Selective-Amnesia

</details>


### [242] [Agentic AI uncovers conserved cross-tissue protein co-abundance programs inaccessible to single-dataset analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28990)
*Runyu Guan,Dehao Wu,Qiqi Xie,Yang Li,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM-agent的框架，用于大规模、证据驱动的组织特异性蛋白共丰度网络比较。对41个人类组织和体液的820个两两组合进行分析，识别出406个组织对中的1,833个保守共丰度簇，发现结肠、滑液、血液、脑脊液和骨髓是最广泛连接的组织，并揭示了皮肤-骨髓、结肠-乳腺等非显而易见的组织间关系及多种机制假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前跨组织研究仅关注生物学预先选定的组织对，大多数可能的组织组合和隐含关系未被探索。需要一种可扩展的方法来系统性地比较组织特异性蛋白共丰度网络，以发现共享的疾病机制和候选治疗靶点，特别是当器官局限性疾病中涉及的蛋白在外周或可及组织中汇聚时。

Method: 构建一个LLM-agent框架：(1) 构建组织特异性蛋白共丰度网络；(2) 推导两两组织间的共识簇；(3) 整合来自表达图谱、蛋白互作与复合物数据库、通路注释、疾病目录和文献的多源证据。框架应用于41个人类组织和体液的820个两两组合。

Result: 识别出406个组织对中的1,833个保守共丰度簇。结肠、滑液、血液、脑脊液和骨髓是最广泛连接的组织，骨髓在簇丰富的组织对中占主导地位。发现非显而易见的关联：皮肤-骨髓超过解剖学相邻的骨-骨髓对；结肠-乳腺包含涉及细胞外基质重塑、脂质代谢和免疫调节的癌症相关簇。簇级分析提出脑-肠胞外囊泡/氧化还原/血清素辅因子轴和肝-骨髓应激反应轴等机制假设。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了保守蛋白共丰度的全局可比图谱，是一个用于机制和治疗探索的假设生成资源。代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Protein co-abundance clusters preserved across tissues can reveal shared disease mechanisms and candidate therapeutic targets, particularly when proteins implicated in organ-confined diseases converge in peripheral or accessible tissues. However, previous cross-tissue studies have focused on biologically pre-selected tissue pairs, leaving most possible combinations and non-obvious relationships unexplored. We present an LLM-agent framework for large-scale, evidence-grounded comparison of tissue-specific protein co-abundance networks. The framework constructs tissue networks, derives pairwise consensus clusters, and integrates evidence from expression atlases, protein interaction and complex databases, pathway annotations, disease catalogues, and literature. Applied to all 820 pairwise combinations of 41 human tissues and fluids, it identified 1,833 conserved co-abundance clusters across 406 tissue pairs. Colon, synovial fluid, blood, cerebrospinal fluid, and bone marrow were the most broadly connected tissues, while the most cluster-rich pairs were dominated by bone marrow. The analysis also highlighted non-obvious relationships: skin-bone marrow exceeded the anatomically adjacent bone-bone marrow pair, while colon-breast contained cancer-relevant clusters involving extracellular-matrix remodeling, lipid metabolism, and immune modulation. Cluster-level analyses generated further mechanistic hypotheses, including a brain-gut extracellular-vesicle/redox/serotonin-cofactor axis and a liver-bone marrow stress-response axis involving genes linked to white matter disease. These results provide a global, comparable landscape of conserved protein co-abundance and a hypothesis-generating resource for mechanistic and therapeutic exploration. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Gry1005/AgenticAI-conserved-cross-tissue-protein-co-abundance.

</details>


### [243] [Verification abundance, adjudication scarcity: what happens to mathematical knowledge when proof checking becomes free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28997)
*Maher Kallel,Mohamed El Louadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In May 2026 an OpenAI model produced a counterexample to the Erdős unit distance conjecture. Five mathematicians published a human-verified version the same day, and the result entered the literature within weeks. In August 2026 the same laboratory published ten mathematical and theoretical computer science results, each accompanied by a machine-checkable Lean 4 certificate with no unproved steps. Four weeks later, one remained the subject of an unresolved dispute over whether its formalization meant what it claimed.
  We argue that this difference is structural. We distinguish three layers of verification: derivational validity, which a kernel checks; representational fidelity, whether the formal statement means the intended question; and epistemic significance. Only the first is mechanizable. Making it effectively free therefore does not eliminate verification work but shifts the burden to layers dependent on scarce expert attention.
  Measurements of the August corpus illustrate the shift. The kernel-checked proofs total 20.6 MB, while the statements requiring human audit total 55.6 KB, a ratio of 379 to 1. Yet those statements contain 218 bespoke definitions rather than relying on community-vetted ones. The audit surface is therefore small in volume but irreducibly expert. We argue that machine checking produces verification abundance while leaving adjudication scarce. We propose a six-category taxonomy of representational mismatch, a disclosure schema for machine-generated mathematical claims, and implications for software, cryptography, and regulated decision systems.

</details>


### [244] [Multi-Step Forecasting of Grape Berry Temperature based on LSTM Model with Feed-Forward Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29008)
*Srikanth Gorthi,L. G. Divyanth,Dattatray Bhalekar,Markus Keller,Lav Khot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将前馈注意力机制与长短期记忆网络（LSTM）相结合的模型（FAM-LSTM），用于葡萄浆果温度（Tb）的多步高分辨率预测。基于华盛顿州Prosser地区2023-2024年数据训练、2025年夏季数据验证，FAM-LSTM在15分钟至72小时（288时间步）的预测范围内全面优于LSTM、GRU、RNN和随机森林模型，且结合园内微气候数据可进一步提升长时程预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的葡萄浆果温度预测对于葡萄园热胁迫的及时管理至关重要，现有模型在长时程、高分辨率预测方面的精度不足，需要开发更优的预测框架。

Method: 将前馈注意力机制集成到LSTM网络（FAM-LSTM）中，使用2023至2024年的环境数据训练模型，并以2025年夏季数据进行验证。模型在15分钟至72小时（288个时间步）的多个预测层面上与LSTM、GRU、RNN和随机森林进行对比，并在两种输入场景（田外气象站观测与园内微气候测量）下评估。

Result: FAM-LSTM在所有预测层面和输入场景下均优于基准模型。使用田外气象站数据时，MAE和RMSE分别为0.58-1.70°C和0.65-2.07°C；使用园内微气候观测时性能进一步提升，MAE和RMSE分别为0.51-1.55°C和0.71-1.87°C。误差在白天高峰时段（11:00-18:00）最大，并随预测时程延长而增加。

Conclusion: FAM-LSTM框架为葡萄浆果温度预测提供了稳健的解决方案，可有效支持葡萄园精准热胁迫管理。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of grape berry temperature (Tb) is essential for enabling timely heat stress management in vineyards. In this study, a feed-forward attention mechanism integrated with a Long Short-Term Memory network (FAM-LSTM) was developed and evaluated for multi-step, high-resolution Tb prediction. Models were trained using environmental data from 2023 and 2024 at Prosser, WA, USA, and validated on 2025 summer data. FAM-LSTM was benchmarked against LSTM, GRU, RNN, and Random Forest (RF) across horizons ranging from 15 minutes to 72 hours (288 time steps). Two input scenarios were evaluated: nearest open-field weather station observations and in-vineyard microclimate measurements. FAM-LSTM consistently outperformed all benchmark models across all horizons and input scenarios. Incorporating in-vineyard microclimate data significantly improved forecasting accuracy at longer horizons. Using open-field data, FAM-LSTM achieved MAE and RMSE ranges of 0.58 to 1.70 deg C and 0.65 to 2.07 deg C, respectively. In-vineyard observations further improved performance, with MAE and RMSE in the ranges of 0.51 to 1.55 deg C and 0.71 to 1.87 deg C. Error analysis showed prediction uncertainty was highest during peak daytime periods (11:00 to 18:00) and increased progressively with forecast horizon. Overall, the FAM-LSTM framework offers robust Tb forecasting to support precision heat stress management in vineyards.

</details>


### [245] [Frequency Selective Neural Networks as a Foundation Architecture for Time Series Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29012)
*Hui Huang,Ye Sun,Shiyan Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出频率选择性神经网络（FSNN），通过将先进的信号处理数学显式嵌入神经网络拓扑，构建完全可微的维纳式滤波器组（Wiener-like filter bank），在复数域反向传播的支持下自动发现并隔离任务相关的物理模态，解决了现有深度学习架构（CNN、RNN、Transformer）在处理时间序列时的"频谱失明"问题。FSNN在10个多元UEA数据集上达到77.0%的平均准确率，并在高度不平衡的PTB-XL临床心电图基准上全面领先各项指标，且能直接收敛于物理上有意义的频带（如心脏QRS复合波），兼具高可扩展性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度时序模型（CNN、RNN、Transformer）本质上是"频谱盲"的——它们将连续的物理波动映射到不受约束的空间或离散token空间，导致严重的频谱纠缠，将预测准确性与物理现实解耦，表现为不透明的黑盒。这种架构缺陷使得模型无法直接学习和利用数据中真实存在的物理振荡模态，因此需要一种既能保留深度学习表达能力、又能保证物理可解释性的新型基础架构。

Method: FSNN的核心创新在于将高级信号处理的严格数学显式嵌入神经拓扑结构中。具体而言，引入完全可微的维纳式滤波器组（Wiener-like filter bank），通过复数域反向传播（complex-domain backpropagation）进行端到端优化，使网络能够自主发现并隔离给定任务中精确的物理模态，从而从根本上解决频谱纠缠问题。

Result: 在10个标准多元UEA数据集上达到77.0%的平均准确率，达到最先进的预测性能；在高度不平衡的PTB-XL临床心电图基准上，在所有主要指标上均处于领先地位。尤为关键的是，FSNN不像传统方法那样产生抽象的特征图，而是直接收敛于物理上有意义的频带，例如能够隔离心脏QRS复合波。

Conclusion: FSNN为复杂时间域中的鲁棒模式识别提供了一种高度可扩展、可解释的新范式。它成功证明了深度学习模型可以在不牺牲表达能力的前提下获得物理可解释性，并提供了可直接用于实践的代码。

Abstract: Time-series data across physical and biological domains are fundamentally driven by complex, non-stationary oscillatory modes. While deep learning models, such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Recurrent Neural Networks, and Transformers, have dominated sequential analysis, they remain fundamentally "spectral-blind". By mapping continuous physical waves into unconstrained spatial or discrete token spaces, these architectures suffer from severe spectral entanglement, acting as opaque black boxes that decouple predictive accuracy from physical reality. In this paper, we introduce the Frequency Selective Neural Network (FSNN), pioneering a foundation architecture guaranteeing physical interpretability without sacrificing expressive power of deep learning. FSNN addresses spectral entanglement by explicitly embedding the rigorous mathematics of advanced signal processing into its neural topology. Through a fully differentiable Wiener-like filter bank optimized via complex-domain backpropagation, FSNN autonomously discovers and isolates the precise physical modes of a given task. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FSNN establishes state-of-the-art predictive performance, achieving $77.0\%$ average accuracy on the standard 10 multivariate UEA datasets and leading across all major metrics on the highly imbalanced PTB-XL clinical ECG benchmark. Crucially, in contrast to yielding abstract feature maps, FSNN converges directly on physically meaningful frequency bands, such as isolating the cardiac QRS complex, providing a highly scalable, interpretable paradigm for robust pattern recognition in complex temporal domains. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ad6174hhhh/FSNN.

</details>


### [246] [Disentangling Representation using Attributes-based Gaussian Estimation for Medical Sound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29026)
*Ke Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于属性映射嵌入的分解表示学习框架AGEDR，通过将属性映射为向量并与VAE潜在向量的子集对齐，在最小化互信息的同时实现潜在向量的分解，从而提升分类性能、公平性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽具备强大的特征提取能力，但其在公平性和可解释性方面的不足限制了其在医疗领域的应用推广。现有方法难以同时兼顾分类性能、公平性与可解释性，亟需一种能够将属性信息从潜在表示中分解出来的方法。

Method: AGEDR框架包含三个关键部分：(1) 设计属性映射嵌入(AME)模块，将属性映射为向量并与VAE中部分潜在向量对齐；(2) 通过最小化互信息实现对齐部分与其他潜在向量的分解；(3) 利用VAE潜在向量的均值参数训练分类器。

Result: 大量实验表明AGEDR在分类性能上优于传统分类模型和现有分解表示学习方法；消融实验验证了其分解能力和公平性。源代码已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: AGEDR通过属性引导的潜在空间分解，有效提升了深度模型在医疗场景下的分类性能、可解释性和公平性，为分解表示学习在敏感领域中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has a powerful capability of feature extraction. However, the lack of fairness and interpretability in deep neural networks poses limitations to their adoption in the medical domain. This paper proposes a disentangled representation learning (DisenRL) framework, named the Attributes-based Gaussian Estimation for Disentangled Representation (AGEDR), which incorporates Attribute Mapping Embedding (AME) modules designed to map attributes into vectors and align them with a subset of the latent vectors in a Variational AutoEncoder (VAE). This part of the latent vector will be disentangled from the remaining latent vectors by minimizing mutual information. A classifier is then trained using the mean parameters of the latent vectors from the VAE. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AGEDR outperforms both conventional classification models and existing disentangled representation learning methods. The ablation experiments also indicate the disentangling capability and fairness of AGEDR. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZhaoKe1024/DisentangledRepr.

</details>


### [247] [Facts Without Rules: Boundary Metadata Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29028)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示多智能体LLM系统中"交接工件"（handoff artifact）这一压缩环节是结构性隐私泄漏源：摘要压缩在保留操作事实的同时削弱了边界元数据，作者将这一失效模式称为"摘要坍缩"（summary collapse）。实验表明边界标记与操作事实的存活率几乎不相关，模糊语言导致高泄漏率，而显式约束和受众白名单能大幅降低泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统广泛通过压缩上游交互为交接工件来协调，但交接环节的隐私泄漏问题尚未被系统研究。理解该环节为何泄漏、何时泄漏以及如何缓解，对构建安全的多智能体系统至关重要。

Method: 在受控的多智能体协调测试平台上，使用经人类验证的评判器（κ=0.74）测量标记存活率，定义σ_b指标（1=边界标记完整保留，0=全部丢失）。在GPT-5-mini和DeepSeek-R1-32B上测试不同交接条件（自由文本vs. 25词预算），并进行下游受控测试、单智能体对照实验以及多种缓解策略（提示词缓解、精确字符串红action、受众白名单）的效果评估。

Result: 自由文本交接保留边界σ_b≈0.80，而25词预算将σ_b降至≈0.57，但操作事实存活率仍接近上限；边界与操作事实存活率近乎不相关（Pearson r接近零）。模糊语言在GPT中73%、DeepSeek中50%的情况泄漏，显式约束可将泄漏降至15%以下。单智能体对照表明该问题非多智能体拓扑所致。提示词缓解和字符串红action仅部分有效，而金标准受众白名单几乎完全消除泄漏。

Conclusion: 交接压缩是隐私泄漏的结构性根源，泄漏程度取决于边界信息的显式程度。正确识别受众边界是解决该问题的关键因素，仅靠提示词或简单红action不足以应对，需要将受众信息作为交接工件的一等公民显式传递。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often coordinate by compressing an upstream interaction into a handoff artifact that downstream agents treat as shared state. We show that this handoff step is a structural source of privacy leakage: summaries preferentially preserve operational facts while weakening the boundary metadata that governs how those facts may be used---a failure mode we call \emph{summary collapse}. On a controlled multi-agent coordination testbed we measure marker survival with a human-validated judge ($κ= 0.74$), where $σ_b = 1$ means every boundary marker survives verbatim and $σ_b = 0$ means all are lost. Boundary-marker and operational-fact survival are nearly uncorrelated at the handoff level on both GPT-5-mini and DeepSeek-R1-32B (Pearson $r$ near zero): uncompressed free-text handoffs preserve boundaries at $σ_b \approx 0.80$, whereas a $25$-word budget drops $σ_b$ to ${\approx}0.57$ while operational-fact survival stays near ceiling. Controlled downstream tests reveal that protection depends on \emph{boundary explicitness}: vague languages leak in $73\%$ of GPT and $50\%$ of DeepSeek cases, while explicit constraints reduce leakage to under $15\%$ across all three tested models. A no-handoff single-agent control further shows the failure is not reducible to multi-agent topology as direct full-marker access still leaks more often than the operationalized handoff. Prompt-only mitigation and exact-string redaction only partially address the problem, while a gold-derived audience allowlist nearly eliminates leakage across models, showing that correctly identifying audience boundaries is the key factor.

</details>


### [248] [Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
*Yepeng Liu,Tianyi Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Dawn Song,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ICWBench基准，用于评测LLM在上下文水印（ICW）任务中的可检测性与回答质量双重表现，并提出了无需蒸馏、无需人工标注的两阶段训练方法（SDLP + RL），将Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B的平均TPR@1%FPR分别从0.100提升至0.974、从0.337提升至0.968，同时保持高回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文水印（ICW）通过在查询中前置指令来使LLM在回复中嵌入可统计检测的信号，让第三方无需访问模型内部即可调用水印接口。然而，其可靠性依赖于LLM遵循指令且不损害回答质量，但当前对LLM在这方面的能力尚未有系统评测。

Method: 首先引入ICWBench基准，包含三个可验证的ICW指令家族，分别从可检测性和回答质量两个维度进行评分。随后提出两阶段训练方法：第一阶段为带logits扰动的自蒸馏（SDLP），利用同一基础LLM同时作为教师和学生，通过指令等价的解码时logits扰动使教师遵循ICW指令，学生则训练以匹配教师的输出分布；第二阶段使用自动验证器作为奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 对14个前沿专有和开源LLM的评估显示，没有任何模型能在所有三个指令家族中同时实现两个目标。应用于Qwen3-14B时，平均TPR@1%FPR从0.100提升至0.974；应用于GPT-OSS-20B时，从0.337提升至0.968，且在困惑度评估和LLM作为评判者的评估下均保持高回答质量。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在ICW指令遵循方面表现不足，而所提出的两阶段训练方法能有效提升水印可检测性且不损害回答质量，为ICW的实际部署提供了可行的训练路径。

Abstract: In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.

</details>


### [249] [EmoLASP: Emotion Recognition with Language Models and Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29035)
*Thao Le,Michael Thielscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmoLASP是一个结合语言模型与答案集编程（ASP）声明式推理的框架，用于在对话中预测VAD（效价-唤醒-支配）情感分数。在IEMOCAP基准上对6种开源LLM（3B-120B）和2种PLM（BERT、RoBERTa）的实验表明，即使在LLM/PLM没有任何对话历史的情况下，EmoLASP也能提升预测性能，特别是在零微调的prompt-only LLM上增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话中的情感识别越来越多地使用语言模型，但这些模型微调成本高且不稳定，使用长对话历史进行提示也很昂贵。作者旨在通过引入声明式推理来降低微调成本和提示成本，同时保证情感预测的一致性。

Method: EmoLASP框架将语言模型与Answer Set Programming（ASP）声明式推理相结合，用于预测对话中的VAD分数。该框架对LLM采用prompt-only方式（无需微调），对PLM可进行微调，并通过ASP推理层增强情感预测的一致性。

Result: 在IEMOCAP数据集上，跨越6种LLM（3B-120B参数）和2种PLM的实验显示：EmoLASP相比单独使用语言模型能提升预测性能，即使没有对话历史输入时也有效；增益在prompt-only LLM上最大；对于已微调的PLM，在具备对话历史时推理器带来的增益很小。

Conclusion: EmoLASP展示了结合推理方法在确保情感预测一致性、降低微调成本和长对话历史提示成本方面的潜在优势，为对话情感识别提供了一种更具成本效益的替代方案。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversations is increasingly tackled with language models, but these models can be unstable and expensive to fine-tune or to prompt with long dialogue histories. We propose EmoLASP, a framework that combines a language model with declarative reasoning via Answer Set Programming (ASP) to predict VAD scores (Valence-Arousal-Dominance) in conversations. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset (IEMOCAP) across six open-source LLMs (3B-120B) and two PLMs (BERT, RoBERTa) show that EmoLASP improves prediction performance compared to using the language model alone, even when the LLMs/PLMs are given no dialogue history in their prompts or input vectors. The gains are largest for prompt-only LLMs, which EmoLASP uses without any fine-tuning. However, for fine-tuned PLMs, the reasoner adds little once dialogue history is available. EmoLASP's LLM pipeline demonstrates the potential advantages of using a reasoning approach to ensure emotion prediction consistency and to reduce both the cost of fine-tuning and the cost of prompting with long dialogue histories.

</details>


### [250] [Let Prompts Bridge Defense Knowledge: Transferable Graph Purification via Vulnerability-Aware GPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29054)
*Shuomin Xue,Jingyuan Li,Ju Jia,Jingxuan Yu,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProGAP，一种基于漏洞感知图提示学习的可迁移图净化方案，通过预训练扰动捕获边检测器并设计漏洞感知提示，实现跨域图对抗防御知识的有效迁移，在提升防御性能的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对抗净化方法本质上是域受限防御，存在两个关键问题：(1) 单域数据无法提供足够的结构和语义多样性来学习鲁棒净化准则；(2) 从零训练域特定防御策略消耗大量计算成本。因此需要一种可迁移的图净化方案来跨域共享对抗防御知识。

Method: ProGAP采用两阶段方案：(1) 在数据丰富的源图上预训练一个扰动捕获边检测器，联合建模拓扑与语义信息以捕捉通用对抗模式；(2) 设计漏洞感知提示，将针对性的净化引导注入偏差节点，使预训练检测器无需大量参数更新即可适应下游图分布偏移。

Result: 与最先进的基线方法相比，ProGAP实现了1%-9%的性能提升，同时将时间消耗降低了最多2.2倍。

Conclusion: ProGAP通过可迁移的图净化框架有效解决了域受限防御的局限性，验证了漏洞感知图提示学习在跨域图对抗防御知识迁移中的有效性，兼顾了防御性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a cornerstone for representing complex relational dependencies in diverse multimedia tasks, particularly in cross-platform user interest modeling and cross-modal semantic alignment. In the real world, a practical defense against graph adversarial perturbations is needed. However, we observe that the prevailing adversarial purification methods are essentially domain-restricted defenses, which leads to the following shortcomings: (1) single-domain data provides insufficient structural and semantic diversity for learning robust purification criteria; (2) training of domain-specific defense strategies from scratch consumes substantial computational cost. To address the above limitations, we propose a transferable graph purification scheme, named ProGAP, to bridge adversarial defense knowledge via vulnerability-aware graph prompt learning. Firstly, to capture universal adversarial patterns, a perturbation-capture edge detector is pretrained on data-rich graphs by jointly modeling topological and semantic information. Subsequently, to achieve more knowledge transfer w.r.t. robustness, vulnerability-aware prompts are designed that inject targeted purification guidance into biased nodes, during which the pretrained detector adapts to distribution shifts in downstream graphs without parameter-laborious updates. Experimental results demonstrate that compared with state-of-the-art baselines, our ProGAP achieves 1%-9% improvement, and reduces the time consumption by up to 2.2x. The code for ProGAP is available at https://github.com/Lieyoufffff/ProGAP.

</details>


### [251] [Agent2UCB: Agentic System for Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29063)
*Sheldon Yu,Rui Wang,Tong Yu,Sungchul Kim,Doga Dogan,Junda Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent2UCB，一个智能体驱动的生成引擎优化（GEO）系统，通过基于bandit策略的反馈驱动优化，自主提升内容在LLM驱动的搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews和Perplexity）中的可见性，同时在GEO-Bench上保持SEO质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型驱动的搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews和Perplexity）的出现催生了GEO这一新兴实践，即优化内容以提高被生成系统引用或总结的概率。然而，如何在众多GEO策略中高效选择最有效的方法，并同时监控对传统SEO质量的影响，是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: Agent2UCB系统包含三个核心组件：1）针对每个内容条目评估九种不同的GEO策略；2）采用基于bandit的Agent2UCB策略，将LLM先验知识与在线奖励信号相结合，加速最优策略的筛选与选择；3）提供轻量级、纯文本的SEO就绪度评估工具，涵盖可读性、主题覆盖和EEAT风格可信度，以监控优化带来的副作用。

Result: 在GEO-Bench基准上的实验表明，Agent2UCB在提升内容可见性方面保持一致的效果，同时未损害SEO质量。系统还提供了交互式演示，允许用户选择感兴趣的网站、观察优化工作流程，并比较不同方法下的GEO/SEO结果。

Conclusion: Agent2UCB展示了将智能体自主优化与bandit学习策略相结合来应对GEO的有效性，为在生成式搜索生态下提升内容可见性提供了一种可扩展且可监控的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model driven search engines such as Google AI Overviews and Perplexity have created new opportunities for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) the practice of refining content to increase its likelihood of being cited or summarized by generative systems. We demonstrate Agent2UCB, an agentic GEO system that autonomously improves content visibility through customized, feedback-driven optimization. For each content item, the system evaluates nine GEO strategies, identifies the most effective method, and accelerates selection using a bandit-based Agent2UCB policy that integrates LLM priors with online reward signals. To monitor side effects, the system also provides a lightweight, text-only SEO readiness evaluation covering readability, topical coverage, and EEAT-style credibility. Experiments on GEO-Bench show consistent visibility gains while preserving SEO quality. The demo allows users to choose the websites of interest, observe the optimization workflow, and compare GEO/SEO outcomes across methods.

</details>


### [252] [Revolutionizing Turn-by-Turn Navigation with Cloud-Edge Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29073)
*Yiming Yang,Hao Fu,Fanxiang Zeng,Xikai Yang,Yue Liu,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的逐向（TBT）导航音频指令生成框架，将导航指令生成建模为多任务学习问题，利用Transformer的时空信息处理能力和Mixture of Experts（MoE）的多任务学习能力，结合云边协同架构实现实时上下文感知的音频导航指令生成。真实世界实验表明，该方法相比传统基于规则的方法显著降低了偏航率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TBT导航系统的音频指令策略多依赖基于规则的方法，难以在信息含量与认知负荷之间取得平衡，在复杂环境中容易导致驾驶员困惑或错过转弯。

Method: 1) 将导航指令音频内容分解为模块化元素组合，建模为多任务学习问题；2) 提出结合Transformer和MoE的深度学习框架生成实时、上下文感知的音频指令；3) 实现云边协同架构以处理模型计算需求，保障可扩展性和实时性能。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法相比传统方法显著降低了偏航率（车辆偏离导航路线的比例），提供更清晰、更有效的音频指令。这是深度学习在大规模驾驶音频导航中的首次应用。

Conclusion: 该研究标志着深度学习在驾驶音频导航领域的大规模应用取得实质性突破，为智能交通和驾驶辅助技术带来了显著进步。

Abstract: Turn-by-turn (TBT) navigation systems are integral to modern driving experiences, providing real-time audio instructions to guide drivers safely to destinations. However, existing audio instruction policy often relies on rule-based approaches that struggle to balance informational content with cognitive load, potentially leading to driver confusion or missed turns in complex environments. To overcome these difficulties, we first model the generation of navigation instructions as a multi-task learning problem by decomposing the audio content into combinations of modular elements. Then, we propose a novel deep learning framework that leverages the powerful spatiotemporal information processing capabilities of Transformers and the strong multi-task learning abilities of Mixture of Experts (MoE) to generate real-time, context-aware audio instructions for TBT driving navigation. A cloud-edge collaborative architecture is implemented to handle the computational demands of the model, ensuring scalability and real-time performance for practical applications. Experimental results in the real world demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces the yaw rate (the proportion of vehicles deviating from navigation routes) compared to traditional methods, delivering clearer and more effective audio instructions. This is the first large-scale application of deep learning in driving audio navigation, marking a substantial advancement in intelligent transportation and driving assistance technologies.

</details>


### [253] [Nested Convex-Body Chasing for Online Optimization with Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29074)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究带嵌套收缩可行域的两类在线优化问题：具有嵌套演化可行集的凸优化（CONES）和对偶约束在线凸优化（COCO）。通过将损失控制与几何移动分离，算法在保持遗憾界的同时，利用欧几里得嵌套凸体追踪器将移动量控制在多项式维度依赖范围内，改进了先前分析中O(d^{d/2})的投影路径因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线优化研究中，当可行域随时间嵌套收缩时，算法的移动代价往往很大。传统的投影路径因子在维度上呈指数级增长（O(d^{d/2})），严重限制了高维场景下的应用。本文旨在设计一种将损失控制与几何追踪分离的框架，以多项式维度依赖替代指数级因子，同时保持遗憾和约束违反的保证。

Method: 本文提出"分离式"方法：损失由约束最小化器和累积损失测试控制，几何移动由确定性可重置的嵌套凸体追踪器（chaser）控制。具体包括：(1) 对CONES使用欧几里得追踪比O(√(d log(1+d)))追踪可行集与目标次水平集的交集；(2) 对COCO使用一步延迟追踪结合正则化领导者重置；(3) 在二维情况下对任意随机算法证明下界；(4) 在线性增长条件下使用Steiner点追踪获得与T无关的移动量。

Result: CONES场景下获得无前缀非正遗憾，移动量O(√(d log(1+d)·GD log(eT)/μ))。二维情形下证明任意终端遗憾O(T^β)(β<1)的随机算法在某个确定性嵌套序列上遭受Ω(√log T)的期望移动，验证了水平方向的紧致性。一般凸COCO获得遗憾O(G_f D√(d log(1+d)T))和累计约束违反O(G_g D√(d log(1+d)T))；强凸损失下两者均为O(d log(1+d) log(eT))。投影路径因子从O(d^{d/2})降为多项式维度依赖。

Conclusion: 本文建立的损失-移动分离框架有效解决了嵌套收缩可行域的在线优化问题，将维度依赖从指数级改进为多项式级。下界结果表明在一般情形下√log T的移动依赖是不可避免的，但在线性增长条件下可通过Steiner点追踪实现与时间无关的移动。所提出的归约方法为后续研究提供了新的分析范式。

Abstract: We study online optimization with nested shrinking feasible regions in two settings: convex optimization with nested evolving feasible sets (CONES) and adversarial constrained online convex optimization (COCO). Our algorithms separate loss control from geometric movement: constrained minimizers and cumulative-loss tests preserve regret guarantees, while a deterministic resettable nested convex-body chaser limits movement.
  For CONES with a $G$-Lipschitz, $μ$-strongly convex objective on a diameter-$D$ domain, we chase intersections of the current feasible set with adaptive objective sublevel sets. Using the Euclidean chasing ratio $O(\sqrt{d\log(1+d)})$, we obtain nonpositive regret at every prefix and movement $O(\sqrt{d\log(1+d)\,GD\log(eT)/μ})$. The bound adapts to the increase in the constrained optimum value. In dimension two, with all other parameters fixed, every randomized algorithm with terminal expected regret $O(T^β)$, $β<1$, suffers $Ω(\sqrt{\log T})$ expected movement on some deterministic nested sequence, proving optimal horizon dependence. Under linear growth away from the constrained minimizer set, Steiner-point tracking yields movement independent of $T$.
  For general convex COCO, one-step-delayed chasing with regularized-leader resets gives regret $O(G_fD\sqrt{d\log(1+d)T})$ and cumulative constraint violation $O(G_gD\sqrt{d\log(1+d)T})$. For strongly convex losses, both are $O(d\log(1+d)\log(eT))$ when other parameters are fixed. These reductions replace the $O(d^{d/2})$ projection-path factor in prior analyses by the polynomial dimension dependence of Euclidean nested convex-body chasing.

</details>


### [254] [HANIA: Planner-Guided Multimodal Graph Evidence Selection for Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29088)
*Zafar Ali,Asad Khan,Nimbeshaho Thierry,Nabila Amir,Adam A. Q. Mohammed,Pavlos Kefalas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HANIA是一个规划器引导的多模态图证据问答框架，通过冻结视觉-语言模型提取问题相关视觉证据、构建输入接地多模态图、使用两群有限状态规划器协调描述性和关系性证据，并在ScienceQA上实现了无需目标数据集微调或迭代检索的竞争性问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态问答对噪声、不完整和弱接地证据敏感。长非结构化上下文会引入冗余并鼓励无根据生成，而平坦检索可能忽略多步推理所需的关系。现有方法缺乏对证据的显式结构化规划。

Method: HANIA包含四个关键模块：(1) 使用冻结视觉-语言模型从图像和文本中提取简洁且问题相关的视觉证据，并具备显式弃权机制；(2) 构建输入接地的多模态图；(3) 采用两群有限状态规划器协调描述性证据和关系性证据；(4) 通过覆盖感知剪枝，基于相关性、图置信度、概念覆盖率和模态多样性保留紧凑证据集，最终将选中的段落、视觉陈述和图三元组提供给冻结的指令微调解码器。

Result: 在ScienceQA上进行评估，衡量指标包括答案准确率、证据过滤质量、证据预算敏感性和效率。实验表明，结构化证据规划和紧凑图引导检索可在无目标数据集微调和无迭代检索的情况下取得竞争性多模态问答性能。

Conclusion: HANIA证明了结构化证据规划和紧凑图引导检索是有效的多模态问答方案，能够在不依赖目标数据集微调或迭代检索的前提下达到竞争性结果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal question answering remains sensitive to noisy, incomplete, and weakly grounded evidence. Long unstructured contexts can introduce redundancy and encourage unsupported generation, while flat retrieval may overlook relations needed for multi-step reasoning. We present HANIA, a planner-guided multimodal graph framework for evidence-grounded question answering. HANIA processes the supplied image and text using a frozen vision-language model to extract concise question-relevant visual evidence with explicit abstention. It then constructs an input-grounded multimodal graph and applies a two-group finite-state planner to coordinate descriptive and relational evidence. Coverage-aware pruning retains a compact evidence set based on relevance, graph confidence, concept coverage, and modality diversity. The selected passages, visual statements, and graph triples are provided to a frozen instruction-tuned decoder. We evaluate HANIA on ScienceQA using answer accuracy, evidence-filtering quality, evidence-budget sensitivity, and efficiency. The results show that structured evidence planning and compact graph-guided retrieval can support competitive multimodal question answering without target-dataset fine-tuning or iterative retrieval. The code is available at https://github.com/Zafar-southeast/HANIA.

</details>


### [255] [EviAnchor: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Regional Visual Evidence Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29092)
*Sihang Jia,Shuliang Liu,Songbo Yang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EviAnchor，一种无需训练、单分支的推理框架，通过区域证据锚定（REA）槽渐进聚合密集视觉token，并采用决策条件证据路由强化当前决策状态对视觉锚的访问，以缓解LVLM在生成后期视觉证据衰减导致的幻觉问题，在POPE、CHAIR和MMHal-Bench上一致提升视觉接地性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）经常生成与视觉输入不符的内容（幻觉）。初步实验发现，视觉证据主要在早期至中期的解码器层被整合到答案侧表示中，而在后期层中其直接影响逐渐减弱。这种视觉证据衰减意味着早期获取的视觉信息在后续生成过程中可能未被充分利用，从而导致幻觉问题。

Method: 提出EviAnchor框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 引入区域证据锚定（REA）槽，将密集视觉token渐进聚合为空间结构化的表示；(2) 通过决策条件证据路由，强化当前决策状态对视觉锚的访问，缓解对文本上下文的过度依赖；(3) 恢复原生Transformer计算，将检索到的视觉证据与问题语义及生成历史进行整合。

Result: 在POPE、CHAIR和MMHal-Bench三个基准上一致实现视觉接地性能的提升，且框架无需训练、采用单分支推理结构。

Conclusion: EviAnchor通过保留并在整个生成过程中重新激活视觉证据，有效缓解了LVLM中视觉证据在深层解码器中衰减的问题，从而改善视觉接地能力并减少幻觉。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) frequently generate content unsupported by visual inputs. Preliminary experiments show that visual evidence is primarily incorporated into answer-side representations in early-to-middle decoder layers, while its direct influence progressively weakens in later layers. This attenuation suggests that visual evidence acquired earlier may be insufficiently utilized during subsequent generation. Based on this observation, we propose EviAnchor, a training-free and single-branch inference framework that preserves and reactivates visual evidence throughout generation. EviAnchor introduces Regional Evidence Anchor (REA) slots to progressively aggregate dense visual tokens into spatially structured representations. It then strengthens the current decision state's access to these visual anchors through decision-conditioned evidence routing, mitigating excessive dependence on textual context. Finally, the model resumes its native Transformer computation to integrate the retrieved visual evidence with question semantics and generation history. Experiments across POPE, CHAIR, and MMHal-Bench demonstrate consistent improvements in visual grounding.

</details>


### [256] [SafeAtlas-VL: Beyond Binary Multimodal Safety with Large-Scale Data and Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29098)
*Zongrui Wang,Xiangyang Zhu,Sicheng Wang,Han Wang,Dingyi Rong,Zeyu Zhang,Chunyi Li,Yue Shi,Kaiwei Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Qi Jia,Yan Teng,Wei Sun,Ning Liu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeAtlas-VL数据集（150万训练样本）和SafeAtlas-Bench基准（5000个测试样本），将多模态安全审核从二元决策扩展为五级有序尺度，并跨图像、请求和响应三个层级进行判断。基于该数据集训练的SafeAtlas Guard系列模型通过目标条件化微调和软累积序数头实现五级分类和连续风险评分，其中8B模型在F1分数上超越此前最优约4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全审核机制通常针对单一判断目标训练，且将安全评估简化为二元决策，导致风险无法在多模态交互中跨层级比较，模糊案例也被掩盖。作者旨在解决这一粒度不足和判断目标单一的问题。

Method: 构建150万训练实例的SafeAtlas-VL数据集，覆盖15个危害类别和55个细分子类别，融合真实世界和合成数据源，采用分歧感知的标注流程。在此基础上构建5000个实例的SafeAtlas-Bench测试集。训练SafeAtlas Guard系列模型，采用目标条件化调优（target-conditioned tuning）实现多模态安全检测，并通过软累积序数头（soft cumulative ordinal head）将五级分类映射为连续风险分数。

Result: SafeAtlas Guard模型表现出强泛化能力，即使不使用其他基准的训练集，也能在相应测试集上取得具有竞争力的性能。其中8B模型取得整体最优表现，在F1分数上超越此前最优方法约4%。

Conclusion: 多层级、五级有序的安全审核范式能更细致地评估多模态交互中的风险，SafeAtlas-VL数据集和SafeAtlas Guard模型为多模态安全审核提供了更全面的解决方案，代码、数据和模型均已开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multimodal safety moderation requires distinguishing risks arising from visual content, user intent, and assistant behavior. Existing safeguards, however, are typically trained for a single judgment target and reduce safety assessment to a binary decision. Consequently, risk becomes difficult to compare across a multimodal interaction, and ambiguous cases are obscured. We introduce SafeAtlas-VL, a dataset of 1.5M training instances that places image-, request-, and response-level judgments on a five-level ordered scale. We curate a broad collection of safety-relevant data from both real-world and synthetic sources and apply a disagreement-aware annotation procedure. The resulting dataset spans 15 harm categories and 55 fine-grained subcategories, covering a broad range of multimodal safety scenarios. We also construct SafeAtlas-Bench, a held-out set of 5,000 instances for evaluating five-level predictions and continuous risk scores. Upon this dataset, we train the SafeAtlas Guard series of models via target-conditioned tuning for multimodal safety detection. Our models not only perform five-way classification of safety levels but also map safety to continuous scores through a soft cumulative ordinal head. Experimental results demonstrate that guard models trained on our dataset exhibit strong generalization: even without using the training sets of other benchmarks, they achieve competitive performance on the corresponding test sets. Notably, our 8B model attains the overall best performance, outperforming the previous SOTA by approximately 4% in F1 score. Code, data, and models are released to support further research. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive, harmful, graphic, or disturbing.

</details>


### [257] [Clustering as Approximation by Constrained Projectors: Theory and Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29102)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架，证明k-means、模糊c均值、核k-means、核FCM和谱聚类等广泛的聚类方法都可以表示为作用于信号导出矩阵上的结构化低秩投影算子，并建立统一的优化模板min ||M - M P_B||_F²，揭示了硬投影、模糊投影、核诱导投影和正交投影之间的代数联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法（如k-means、模糊c均值、谱聚类等）虽然在实践中广泛应用，但缺乏统一的数学理论框架来理解它们之间的深层联系。不同的聚类方法往往被视为独立的技术，难以从统一的理论视角进行分析和比较，这阻碍了理论理解和算法设计的系统性进展。

Method: 将各种聚类方法统一建模为min_{B属于C} ||M - M P_B||_F²的优化形式，其中M是信号导出矩阵，P_B是低秩投影算子，C是不同方法的约束集。在这个框架下，推导了投影流形上的测地凸性、针对矩阵噪声的扰动界、以及理想块模型条件下的精确恢复保证等理论结果，并分析了不同聚类族收敛到同一最优子空间的条件以及簇间泄漏导致的偏差。

Result: 建立了统一聚类理论框架，推导出测地凸性性质、稳定性扰动界和精确恢复保证等非平凡理论结果，并解释了不同聚类方法何时产生相同的最优子空间以及小簇间泄漏下的行为差异。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过结构化投影理解聚类提供了连贯的、理论优先的基础，首次将多种主流聚类方法统一在一个代数框架下，为未来聚类算法的理论分析和设计提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified theoretical framework showing that a broad family of clustering methods, including k-means, fuzzy c-means, kernel k-means, kernel FCM, and spectral clustering, can all be expressed as structured low-rank projectors acting on a signal-derived matrix. By formulating each method as an instance of min over B in C of ||M - M P_B||_F^2, with different constraint sets C, we establish a common optimization template that clarifies the algebraic links among hard, fuzzy, kernel-induced, and orthonormal projections. Within this framework, we derive non-trivial theoretical results, including geodesic convexity properties on the projection manifold, perturbation bounds quantifying stability to matrix noise, and exact recovery guarantees under ideal block-model conditions. The analysis further explains when different clustering families collapse to the same optimal subspace and how deviations arise under small inter-cluster leakage. Overall, the work provides a coherent, theory-first foundation for understanding clustering through structured projectors.

</details>


### [258] [Emergent Misalignment Is Not Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29118)
*Mingxuan Li,Qirun Dai,Heran Wang,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）中的"涌现性错位"（Emergent Misalignment, EM）是一种可预测的、数据依赖的泛化现象，而非意外行为。通过分析基础模型对训练数据和评估提示的表示距离，发现模型在EM训练后的"邪恶程度"与评估提示到训练数据质心的表示距离高度相关（平均Spearman相关系数为-0.73）。论文还驳斥了"通用错位方向"和"邪恶人格"的既有解释框架，并提出了一个数据集特定的泛化方向度量，能够稳健预测模型在语义保持扰动下的错位程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM现象对AI安全构成挑战，但先前的解释框架（通用错位方向、邪恶人格）机制模糊不清。作者旨在从基础模型的表征空间出发，揭示EM背后的可预测规律，并提供更可靠的泛化度量方法。

Method: （1）计算EM训练数据与评估提示在基础模型表征空间中的距离，并使用Spearman相关性检验距离与错位程度的关系；（2）通过改变训练数据格式验证EM有效性的敏感性；（3）检验是否存在跨模型通用的错位方向；（4）将标量距离推广为数据集特定的泛化方向向量，用于预测语义保持扰动（如随机token追加、改写）下的错位程度。

Result: 在12个模型-数据集组合下，表示距离与错位程度平均Spearman相关系数为-0.73；EM效果显著依赖于训练数据格式；不存在跨EM模型的通用错位方向；EM效应与人格改变有本质区别。提出的数据集特定泛化方向方法在语义保持扰动下比其他方法更稳健地预测EM模型的错位程度。

Conclusion: EM并非不可预测的意外行为，而是可由基础模型表征空间中的表示距离解释的数据依赖泛化现象。通过理解其表征机制并提出数据集特定的泛化方向度量，可以更准确地预测和控制EM，为AI安全提供新的理论工具。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly harmful datasets can lead to misalignment broadly, a phenomenon known as emergent misalignment (EM). EM poses a challenge for AI safety and our understanding of LLMs. Prior work often frames EM as an unexpected behavior, and explains it by appealing to general misalignment directions or anthropomorphizing it as acquiring an evil persona. However, the mechanisms behind these framings remain obscure. In this work, we show that EM is a predictable and data-dependent generalization phenomenon. By examining the base model's representation of EM training data and evaluation prompts, we find that evilness after EM training is highly predictable from representational distance: the closer an evaluation prompt is to training data centroid, the more evilness it elicits from EM models after training (with an average Spearman correlation of -0.73 across 12 model-dataset settings). Building upon this analysis, we further demystify EM by showing that (1) its effectiveness changes significantly based on training data format; (2) there is not a general misalignment direction that transfers across different EM models; (3) the effect of EM is fundamentally different from persona changes. Furthermore, we extend the EM generalization metric from a scalar distance to a dataset-specific generalization direction, which robustly predicts EM models' evilness under semantics-preserving prompt perturbations including appending random tokens and paraphrasing, where other methods do not reliably generalize.

</details>


### [259] [Beyond Correctness: Validity-Oriented Evaluation of Biomedical LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29127)
*Rodrigo de Oliveira,Federico Pittino,James Gwinnutt,Jay Nanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展、面向有效性验证的医学LLM评判器评估管线，在人类标注稀缺时通过确定性突变增强基准数据，并从正确性、鲁棒性和格式合规性三个维度评估评判器。实验表明SFT→RL两阶段训练优于基线、单阶段SFT或RL，在PICO提取和临床计算等可分解任务上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的人类评判数据在生物医学领域十分稀缺且昂贵，导致对医学LLM评判器（judges）的评估缺乏可扩展且有效的验证手段。现有方法通常仅关注聚合正确性指标，忽略部署中的鲁棒性和格式合规性等关键维度。

Method: 提出两阶段评估管线：(1) 对现有带人类标注的生物医学基准数据进行基于确定性指标（metric-grounded）的突变增强，生成可审计（auditable）的偏好对；(2) 从三个部署相关维度评估评判器——基于指标推导的金标正确性、随机采样的鲁棒性、输出格式合规性。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上比较四种训练策略：base、SFT、GRPO强化学习、SFT→RL。

Result: 基线模型和单阶段训练（SFT或RL）在结构化医学判别任务（如PICO提取、临床计算）上表现不佳；SFT→RL在正确性、合规性和鲁棒性三个维度均表现最优，其增益集中在可分解任务（PICO、MedCalc）上，有时达到或超越前沿模型水平。

Conclusion: SFT→RL两阶段训练范式是提升医学LLM评判器能力最有效的策略，尤其在可分解的医学判别任务上。所提出的增变-多维度评估管线为人类标注稀缺场景下验证医学评判器提供了可行且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: We propose a scalable, validity-oriented pipeline for evaluating biomedical LLM judges when high-quality human judgments are scarce. First, we augment existing human-labelled biomedical benchmarks with deterministic, metric-grounded mutations that produce auditable preference pairs. Second, we evaluate judges beyond aggregate correctness using three deployment-relevant dimensions: correctness against metric-derived gold labels, robustness under repeated stochastic sampling, and compliance with the requested output format. We use this pipeline to assess Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four regimes: (1) base, using the instruct model as is; (2) SFT, distillation-based supervised fine-tuning only; (3) RL, GRPO-based reinforcement learning only; and (4) SFT$\rightarrow$RL, SFT followed by RL. The base and single-stage regimes struggle on structured medical discrimination such as PICO extraction and clinical calculations, whereas SFT$\rightarrow$RL performs best across correctness, compliance, and robustness; gains concentrate on decomposable tasks (PICO, MedCalc), at times matching or outperforming frontier models.

</details>


### [260] [APIFlow-Bench: Measuring Whether Agents Survive Long, Dependent API Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29128)
*Zelin Wan,Arash Nourian,Xiaoxiao Li,Nihar Nandan,Kamalakannan Nandagopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出APIFlow-Bench，一个可完全审计的长时程、依赖型REST-API工作流基准测试，将端到端性能分解为七项工程能力，并通过状态追踪与逐字段验证实现确定性、溯源敏感的评分。在19个前沿与开源模型上的实验揭示了三个关键发现：更长的依赖链降低成功率、可靠性比最佳能力更能区分模型、独立错误假设无法解释复合失败现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具使用智能体评估仅使用端到端工作流是否完成这个单一比特来衡量，无法区分生产中真正重要的失败类型（如凭据过期、负载格式错误、正确执行但最终交付错误）。这种粗粒度评估掩盖了不同子能力的缺陷，无法指导实际部署与改进。

Method: APIFlow-Bench采用正向逐子任务生成合成API世界：每个子任务需通过零LLM自检三元组（校验其评分器）并由oracle建立可解性，同时通过对抗性审计识别并修复了6个评分器漏洞。评分是确定性和溯源敏感的：状态检查通过模拟铸造的canary令牌追踪API数据流到答案必须来源的响应，类型化答题卡逐字段验证。共发布44,362份未删减执行记录。

Result: 在19个前沿与开放权重模型、统一中立脚手架下发现：(1) 依赖链变长时成功率从单个子任务的93%降至干净20子任务链的74%，若包含共识筛选标记为无模型通过的8%链试验则降至61%；(2) 可靠性比最佳能力更能区分模型——五次最好成绩横跨7个百分点，而五次全成功可靠性横跨44个百分点；(3) 独立错误解释模型不成立：20子任务链的通过率比子任务级通过率的乘积高33个百分点，且干净切片中77%失败运行到达正确的最终状态，仅失败在交付环节。

Conclusion: APIFlow-Bench表明依赖链的长度对智能体成功率有显著影响，可靠性与最佳能力是孑然不同的评估维度，且长链失败主要是交付环节问题而非逐个子任务的独立错误累积。该基准提供了一个更细粒度、可审计的评估框架，为生产环境部署工具使用智能体提供了诊断依据。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are commonly evaluated by a single bit: whether an end-to-end workflow completed. This metric fails to distinguish failures that matter in production, such as expired credentials, malformed payloads, or correct execution followed by incorrect final delivery. We introduce APIFlow-Bench, a fully auditable benchmark for long-horizon, dependent REST-API workflows that decomposes performance into seven engineering capabilities and requires agents to produce answers supported by the actual call path. We generate synthetic API worlds forward, subtask by subtask; each subtask is admitted only after a zero-LLM self-test triad verifies its grader and an oracle establishes solvability, and an adversarial audit identified and fixed six grader exploits. Grading is deterministic and provenance-sensitive: a state check traces a mock-minted canary through the API data flow to the response the answer must originate from, and a typed answer card is verified field by field. We release all answer keys and 44,362 unredacted execution transcripts. Across 19 frontier and open-weight models under one neutral scaffold, we find: (1) longer dependency chains degrade success, from 93% on individual subtasks to 74% on clean 20-subtask chains and 61% when including the 8% of chain trials that a model-consensus screen flags as passed by no model; (2) reliability separates models more than best-case capability, with best-of-five spanning seven points but all-five-of-five reliability spanning 44 points; (3) the independent-error account of compounding failure does not fit the data: pass rates on 20-subtask chains are 33 percentage points above the product of subtask-level rates, and on the clean slice 77% of failing runs reached the correct final state and failed only at delivery.

</details>


### [261] [More Perspectives, Stronger Signals: Multi-Perspective Enhancement and Progressive Fusion for Multimodal Entity Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29139)
*Chenyi Xiong,Yan Zhang,Jing Hu,Ziyue Qin,Kui Xiao,Xiaopan Lyu,Xiaoju Hou,Zhifei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PrismF统一框架，通过多视角增强（multi-perspective enhancement）与渐进式融合（progressive fusion）解决多模态知识图谱补全（MMKGC）中模态内语义过度平滑与模态间噪声过滤不足的问题，在三个公开基准上取得最优性能（KVC16K上MRR提升4.04%，Hits@1提升11.17%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态实体表示学习对多模态知识图谱补全等推理任务至关重要，但现有方法在稀疏或模糊条件下存在模态内语义过度平滑以及跨模态噪声过滤无效的问题，限制了模型从多样化输入中提取强信号的能力。

Method: PrismF包含两大核心组件：（1）多视角机制，将每个模态分解为互补视图，并通过解耦损失（decoupling loss）约束以减少表示坍缩，增强细粒度的模态内语义；（2）渐进式融合策略，通过动态校准跨模态交互，使模型更关注信息丰富的信号同时抑制噪声或不可靠信息。

Result: 在三个公开基准上的大量实验表明PrismF达到最强综合性能，其中在KVC16K数据集上MRR相对提升4.04%，Hits@1相对提升11.17%。代码已开源（https://github.com/HubuKG/PrismF）。

Conclusion: PrismF通过多视角增强与渐进式融合的协同设计，有效缓解了模态内语义坍缩与模态间噪声干扰问题，为多模态知识图谱补全提供了一条统一且有效的解决路径。

Abstract: Learning effective multimodal entity representations is fundamental for reasoning tasks such as multimodal knowledge graph completion (MMKGC). However, existing methods often suffer from semantic over-smoothing within modalities and ineffective noise filtration across modalities, particularly under sparse or ambiguous conditions. To overcome these limitations, we propose PrismF, a unified framework that synergizes multi-perspective enhancement with progressive fusion to extract stronger signals from diverse inputs. PrismF enhances fine-grained intra-modal semantics through a multi-perspective mechanism that decomposes each modality into complementary views and constrains them with a decoupling loss to reduce representation collapse. Furthermore, it improves cross-modal integration through a progressive fusion strategy that dynamically calibrates inter-modal interactions, enabling the model to emphasize informative signals while suppressing noisy or unreliable ones. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks show that PrismF achieves the strongest overall performance, including relative improvements of 4.04% in MRR and 11.17% in Hits@1 on KVC16K. Our code can be found at https://github.com/HubuKG/PrismF.

</details>


### [262] [JudgePanel: A Compact Judge with Panel Deliberation via Adaptive Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29168)
*Yiyue Qian,Shinan Zhang,Huan Song,Hannah Marlowe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JudgePanel框架，将多智能体评审推演能力蒸馏进单个紧凑的Judge模型，通过AdaReward自适应多重奖励强化学习提升判断质量，以单模型推理成本实现优于70B模型的评审性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式虽可扩展，但单模型判断受固有模型偏差限制；多智能体评审协议虽能通过多样化讨论缓解偏差，却在推理时成本过高。需要在保持单一模型推理效率的同时获得多智能体协作的评审质量。

Method: (1) 从多个强评估器生成的评审推演轨迹（讨论、分歧、解决的结构化模式）上训练紧凑的Judge模型；(2) 提出AdaReward自适应多重奖励强化学习算法，动态重新平衡各奖励成分的权重，应对不同目标在RL训练中饱和速率不同的问题；(3) 设计轻量级领域特化模块，用数百个标注样本即可快速适应新评估领域。

Result: 基于14B骨干的JudgePanel在四个评估基准上超越最高达70B的评判特化模型，展现出强位置一致性（position consistency），并能快速特化到新领域。

Conclusion: JudgePanel是首个以单模型推理成本赋予紧凑Judge模型多智能体小组评审能力的框架，兼具新颖性、有效性与可靠性，为LLM评估提供了高质量且高效的新范式。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative to human evaluation. However, single-model judges are limited by their inherent model biases, while multi-agent evaluation protocols that mitigate this through diverse deliberation are prohibitively expensive at inference time. To this end, we propose \textbf{\modelname}, which equips a compact \underline{Judge} model with multi-agent \underline{Panel} deliberation capability. Specifically, we first train on panel deliberation traces from an ensemble of strong evaluators, capturing structured patterns of discussion, disagreement, and resolution. To further improve judgment quality beyond SFT, we introduce \textit{AdaReward}, an adaptive multi-reward RL algorithm that dynamically rebalances reward component weights as different objectives saturate at different rates during RL training. For practical deployment, we further design a lightweight domain specialization module for rapid adaptation to new evaluation domains with few hundred labeled samples. As a result, (i) \textit{Novel}: the first framework to equip a single compact judge with multi-agent panel deliberation capability at single-model inference cost; (ii) \textit{Effective \& Reliable}: JudgePanel with a 14B backbone outperforms judge-specialized models up to 70B across four evaluation benchmarks, demonstrates strong position consistency, and rapidly specializes to new domains with few hundred samples.

</details>


### [263] [An Explainable Coherence Score for Detecting Temporal Inconsistencies in Political News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29175)
*Marius Nicusor Pantea,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时间一致性评分（TCS），一种用于检测新闻文章中时间错乱（如时间归因错误、事件时间倒置、因果序列反转）的连续可解释指标，通过四阶段流程（时间事实提取、时间知识图谱构建、层级化验证、评分聚合）实现，在100篇注入时间错误的样本文本上达到0.909的精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于风格的虚假新闻检测器无法识别时间不一致类政治虚假信息——一篇措辞良好但仅含一个错误日期的文章在词汇层面毫无造假信号，需要专门针对时间维度的事实核查方法。

Method: 提出四阶段流水线：1）提取时间事实；2）构建时间知识图谱；3）结合8个基于Allen区间代数的内部检查器与5级外部参考层次（从本地1,256条精选政治事实知识库到实时Wikidata SPARQL查询）进行层级化验证；4）聚合评分并自动生成解释。

Result: 在100篇注入时间错误的新闻文章基准上，系统在选择的工作阈值下达到0.909的精确率，仅有一个残留假阳性，该配置文件刻意调优以支持人机协同事实核查。

Conclusion: 与仅输出二分类标签的词法基线不同，每个被标记的文章都会附带不一致类型、涉及的实体以及与之矛盾的外部参考来源，该设计旨在优先减少误报而非漏报，服务于人工参与的事实核查场景。

Abstract: Temporal inconsistencies, such as mandates attributed outside their real interval, events presented as past before they occurred, or inverted causal sequences, are a form of political disinformation that evades style-based fake news detectors: a well-written article with a single wrong date carries no lexical signal of falsehood. This paper introduces the Temporal Coherence Score (TCS), a continuous, intrinsically interpretable metric that quantifies the temporal coherence of a news article, computed by a four-stage pipeline: extraction of temporal facts, construction of a temporal knowledge graph, hierarchical verification against internal consistency rules and external reference sources, and score aggregation with automatically generated explanations. Verification combines eight internal checkers derived from Allen's interval algebra with a five-level external hierarchy ranging from a locally stored reference knowledge base of 1{,}256 curated political facts to live Wikidata SPARQL queries. On a benchmark of 100 political news articles with injected temporal errors, the system reaches a precision of 0.909 at the selected operating threshold, with a single residual false positive, a profile deliberately tuned for human-in-the-loop fact-checking assistance, where false alarms are costlier than missed detections. Unlike lexical baselines that output only a binary label, every flagged article is accompanied by the inconsistency type, the entities involved, and the reference source that contradicts the claim.

</details>


### [264] [How Identity and Opinion Shape Political Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29198)
*Li-Ni Fu,Chang-Chih Meng,Chien-Hua Chen,Hen-Hsen Huang,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架，区分两种引发语言模型政治谄媚行为的触发因素——观点（对齐显式叙述）和身份（基于人口统计标签的刻板印象）。通过450个手工检验的政治困境作为受控探针，评估13个指令微调LLM，发现模型对观点和身份线索的敏感性是可分离的、效应亚加性的，且系统级人设主要改变基线立场而非影响用户信息引发的立场偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的政治行为基准多依赖封闭式问题，未能充分捕捉模型在交互中随用户提供情境而调整立场的能力，因此需要更精细的框架来解构政治谄媚行为的不同触发机制。

Method: 提出一个解耦框架，将政治谄媚的触发因素分为观点（对齐显式叙事）和身份（基于人口统计标签的刻板印象）两类。使用450个手工核查的政治困境作为受控探针，评估13个指令微调LLM。

Result: 发现观点敏感性与身份线索敏感性之间存在解离，二者不能互相预测；当两种信号同时出现时效应呈亚加性；系统级人设主要改变基线立场，对用户观点或身份引发的立场偏移影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM的政治立场是交互式、可被引导的脆弱特质，而非固定属性；个性化服务可能放大身份或观点条件化的行为偏移。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly encourage users to disclose personal profiles for tailored assistance, measuring their political alignment becomes increasingly important. However, many existing benchmarks for assessing political behavior rely on closed-ended questions and do not fully capture how a model's stance may adapt to user-provided context during interaction. We introduce a framework that disentangles two distinct triggers of political sycophancy: opinion (aligning with explicit narratives) and identity (stereotyping based on demographic labels). Using 450 manually-checked political dilemmas as controlled probes, we evaluate 13 instruction-tuned LLMs. We uncover a dissociation: a model's susceptibility to explicit opinions does not necessarily predict its susceptibility to identity cues, and vice versa. When both signals are present, their effects are generally sub-additive rather than simply additive. Additionally, system-level personas primarily shift a model's baseline stance while having limited effect on the stance shift caused by user opinion or identity. Ultimately, our results suggest that LLM political stance is interactively and steerably vulnerable rather than being a fixed trait, highlighting how personalization may amplify identity- or opinion-conditioned shifts in the model's behaviors.

</details>


### [265] [Benevolent Bias in Multi-Turn Human-Agent Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29206)
*Qianqi Liu,Jin Huang,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了人机交互中"仁慈偏见"（benevolent bias）的概念，即用温暖积极语气掩盖的不平等对待。作者构建了包含362,880个多轮支持对话的均衡语料库BENEVDIAL，并测试了现成安全检测器和LLM裁判两类检测器，发现现成检测器能识别明显偏见但遗漏仁慈偏见，而LLM裁判在更明确标准下能捕捉更多但会产生更多误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中的偏见不仅表现为敌对语言，还可能以"仁慈偏见"形式存在——即以温暖、积极的口吻隐藏不平等对待。这类隐性偏见难以被现有检测方法发现，需要对其进行形式化定义并构建基准数据集来评估检测能力。

Method: 首先从语气（tone）和对待（treatment）两个维度对仁慈偏见进行形式化定义，划分出中性支持、明显偏见、仁慈偏见三类；据此构建BENEVDIAL语料库（含362,880个多轮支持对话，覆盖用户/代理人口统计特征、角色和生成器）；设计两类检测器——现成安全检测器与提示LLM裁判——进行对比评估。

Result: 两类检测器均存在检测缺口：现成安全检测器能可靠识别明显偏见但大多漏掉仁慈偏见；LLM裁判在更明确的检测标准下能捕捉更多仁慈偏见，但会越来越多地将中性支持误分类为仁慈偏见，且人口统计背景信息会放大误报率。

Conclusion: 人机对话的公平监测不能仅依赖表面线索（如语气），而应深入考察代理是否对用户实施了不同的对待（disparate treatment）。当前检测工具的局限表明，需要开发超越表层特征、关注对待差异的公平监测方法。

Abstract: Bias in human-agent interaction can manifest not only through hostile language but also as benevolent bias, whereby unequal treatment hides behind a warm, positive tone. To make it detectable, we operationalise benevolent bias along two dimensions, tone and treatment, yielding three classes: neutral support, overt bias, and benevolent bias. Building on these definitions, we construct BENEVDIAL, a class-balanced corpus of 362,880 multi-turn support dialogues spanning user and agent demographics, roles, and generators, to support controlled evaluation. We then test two detector families on it: off-the-shelf safety detectors and prompted large language model (LLM) judges. Our results reveal a detection gap: off-the-shelf detectors reliably flag overt bias yet largely miss benevolent bias, while LLM judges catch more under more explicit detection criteria but increasingly misclassify neutral support as benevolent bias, and demographic context amplifies the false alarms. These findings suggest that fair monitoring of human-agent dialogue must look beyond surface cues to whether the agent's treatment is disparate.

</details>


### [266] [Hyper-Fold: Exploring the Expressive Limit of Sequence-Geometry Learning for Proteins via Hypergraph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29207)
*Yifan Feng,Guanjie Cheng,Shihui Ying,Shaoyi Du,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hyper-Fold，一种秩K可分离卷积骨架，逼近内容-几何双线性交互的表达上限，以消息传递成本实现蛋白质结构建模，并在酶功能预测、折叠分类和配体结合位点检测上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质结构建模的核心计算原语是残基序列内容与三维几何之间的交互。主流几何GNN采用加性消息传递，被证明对内容-几何绑定关系存在盲区，无法充分表达这类二阶交互信息。需要探索该层类的表达极限并设计更强大的编码器。

Method: 首先理论上证明内容-几何外积上的完整双线性算子（所有二阶交互的充分统计量）是表达上限，而加性消息传递无法达到。随后引入Hyper-Fold：一种秩K可分离卷积骨架，将每个半径邻域组织为序列超边和接触超边，通过边条件矩阵值算子进行调制，该算子被分解为K个可学习基算子与几何生成系数的组合，以消息传递的代价逼近表达上限。还提出层次变体Hyper-Fold-Deep及锚定集合预测头Hyper-Fold-Pocket。

Result: 在酶功能预测、折叠分类和配体结合位点检测任务中，Hyper-Fold和Hyper-Fold-Deep在蛋白质特异结构编码器中取得最佳结果。Hyper-Fold-Pocket在UniSite-DS和两个零样本基准上超越UniSite-3D，且无需序列语言模型特征，参数减少68倍，延迟降低4.8倍。

Conclusion: 足够表达力的3D骨架能够恢复融合架构以往从进化尺度预训练中借用的信息，表明双线性内容-几何交互的表达充分性对蛋白质结构建模至关重要，Hyper-Fold以可接受的代价逼近了这一表达上限。

Abstract: Protein structure modeling rests on a single computational primitive: the interaction between what a residue is (sequence content) and where it sits (three-dimensional geometry). What is the expressive limit of this layer class? We show that the complete bilinear operator over content-geometry outer products--the sufficient statistic of all second-order interactions--is the expressive ceiling, while the additive message passing of mainstream geometric GNNs is provably blind to content-geometry binding. We then introduce Hyper-Fold, a rank-K separable convolutional backbone approaching this ceiling at message-passing cost: each radius neighborhood is organized into a sequence hyperedge and a contact hyperedge, modulated by an edge-conditioned matrix-valued operator factorized into K learned basis operators with geometry-generated coefficients. Across enzyme function prediction, fold classification, and ligand binding site detection, Hyper-Fold and its hierarchical variant Hyper-Fold-Deep achieve the best results among protein-specific structure encoders; Hyper-Fold-Pocket, an anchored set-prediction head, surpasses UniSite-3D on UniSite-DS and two zero-shot benchmarks with no sequence language model features, 68x fewer parameters, and 4.8x lower latency--suggesting that a sufficiently expressive 3D backbone recovers information that fusion architectures previously borrowed from evolution-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [267] [Imag-Eval: a language-grounded framework for interpretable Text-to-Image instruction following evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29210)
*Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis,David Jaramillo Duque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Imag-Eval，一个受控基准测试，用于评估T2I模型将组合自然语言指令转化为视觉输出的能力。该基准通过独立变化实例数量和规则组合来解耦语言复杂性与组合难度，包含1,140个提示和8,842条组合规则，评估结果显示组合难度主要由规则数量及其与实例的绑定决定，而非提示长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型评估存在两个关键缺陷：一是基于技能的分类评估忽视了全局不连贯等关键失败模式（如缺失部分、物理上不可能的配置如漂浮物体）；二是提示难度的控制通常仅沿单一维度（提示长度或生成元素数量），无法解耦表面语言复杂度与真实的组合难度。

Method: Imag-Eval通过独立地变化实例数量（number of instances）和约束组合（combination of rules）两个维度来创建受控基准，从而将表面语言复杂度与组合难度解耦，同时避免错误传播。基准包含1,140个提示和8,842条组合规则，并在多个最先进的T2I模型上进行了评估，同时辅以对并发基准中2,000多个提示的额外研究。

Result: 评估结果表明，对于结构化技能而言，组合难度主要由接地规则（grounded rules）的数量及其与实例的绑定所决定，而非仅由提示长度决定。

Conclusion: Imag-Eval提供了更细粒度、可解释的T2I模型跨模态指令遵循失败分析框架，揭示了组合难度的本质来源，为未来T2I模型的诊断性评估和改进提供了依据。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models have recently achieved impressive visual fidelity, yet their evaluation remains constrained by benchmarks that are often difficult to interpret and insufficiently diagnostic. Existing skill-based evaluations tend to overlook critical failure modes that strongly impact usability but fall outside standard taxonomies, such as global incoherence arising from missing parts or physically implausible configurations (e.g., floating objects). In addition, prompt difficulty is typically controlled along a single dimension; either prompt length or the number of elements to generate. To address these limitations, we introduce Imag-Eval, a controlled benchmark designed to assess how T2I models ground compositional natural-language instructions into visual outputs. Unlike prior work that conflates surface linguistic complexity with compositional difficulty, Imag-Eval explicitly seeks to disentangles these factors by independently varying both the number of instances and the combination of constraints (rules), while avoiding error propagation. This design enables fine-grained and interpretable analysis of where cross-modal instruction following fails. Our benchmark comprises 1,140 prompts and 8,842 combined rules, and we evaluate it on several state-of-the-art models. Complementing this analysis with an additional study of over 2,000 prompts from a concurrent benchmark, our results suggest that, for structured skills, compositional difficulty is primarily governed by the number of grounded rules and their binding to instances,, rather than by prompt length alone.

</details>


### [268] [Computational Depth Measurement in Thermographic Video: Overcoming Spatial Overfitting via Spatio-Temporal Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29223)
*Zain Ul Abidin,Habeeban Memon,Junaid Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空解耦架构用于CFRP复合材料脉冲热成像的亚表层分层深度测量，通过空间分割定位缺陷区域、时序特征回归测量深度，并证明正则化XGBoost在跨样本交叉验证中达到MAE 0.056 mm的精度，有效避免模型对校准缺陷几何布局的空间记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学脉冲热成像仅提供二维热视频而非体数据，需从时间热扩散响应推断分层深度。然而校准缺陷按规则网格排列时，回归模型可能记忆缺陷几何而非学习热衰减与深度之间的物理关系，导致空间数据集偏差和校准崩溃。

Method: 提出时空解耦架构：首先用分割方法定位缺陷区域，然后将热响应空间平均并转化为16个物理信息特征（时序、能量、统计和几何类），仅向深度模型输入一维热传导特征而隐藏像素坐标。评估四种回归模型（RF、GBM、Adv-MLP、XGBoost）并使用样本级交叉验证。

Result: 未正则化的树模型和过参数化Adv-MLP在几何偏移下出现校准崩溃，误差超过0.5 mm；而带L1/L2惩罚和列采样的正则化XGBoost保持跨样本校准，MAE为0.056 mm、RMSE为0.085 mm。预测深度与掩膜融合，可在每样本3-5秒内生成Delaunay三角剖分的三维缺陷模型。

Conclusion: 数学正则化和时空解耦能有效减少热视频深度回归中的空间记忆，提升跨样本泛化能力，实现准确且快速的CFRP分层深度测量与三维缺陷重建。

Abstract: Accurate through-thickness measurement of subsurface delamination depth in Carbon Fiber Reinforced Polymer (CFRP) is important for structural assessment because defect location determines affected load-bearing layers. Optical pulsed thermography (OPT) provides a two-dimensional thermal video rather than volumetric measurements, so depth must be inferred from temporal heat-diffusion responses. A challenge is spatial dataset bias: when calibration defects follow regular grids, regression models may memorize their geometry instead of learning physical relationship between thermal decay and depth. This work introduces a spatio-temporal decoupling architecture that separates spatial defect localization from temporal depth measurement. Defect regions are first localized using segmentation methods, after which thermal responses are spatially averaged and converted into sixteen physics-informed temporal, energy, statistical, and geometric features. These features expose the one-dimensional heat-conduction relationship while withholding pixel coordinates from the depth model. Four regression models are evaluated using specimen-level cross-validation: Random Forest (RF), Gradient Boosting Machine (GBM), Advanced Multi-Layer Perceptron (Adv-MLP), and XGBoost. Unregularized trees and over-parameterized Adv-MLP exhibit calibration collapse under geometric shifts, with errors exceeding 0.5 mm. In contrast, regularized XGBoost with L1/L2 penalties and column sampling maintains cross-specimen calibration, achieving a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.056 mm and root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.085 mm. Predicted depths are merged with masks to generate Delaunay-triangulated three-dimensional defect models in three to five seconds per specimen. Results show that mathematical regularization and spatio-temporal decoupling reduce spatial memorization in thermal-video depth regression.

</details>


### [269] [Localizing Emergent Failures in Agentic AI: Recovering Minimal Repair Families via Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29228)
*Bingjie Li,Yumeng Song,Zhongming Yao,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MRFR问题（最小修复族恢复）用于多LLM智能体系统的故障归因，并提出GCJR方法，通过执行依赖图切片构造候选事件集并经回放验证，在90例基准上达到1.000的家族精确匹配，同时将平均回放调用次数从56.3降至25.3（减少55.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统失败可能源于多个LLM智能体间消息交互的复杂组合效应。逐点归因无法区分联合必要修复与单个替代修复，因此需要恢复所有包含最小的事件集合（修复族）来全面定位故障根源。

Method: 提出MRFR形式化问题：恢复所有在给定大小约束内、通过反事实回放可恢复任务成功的包含最小事件集。提出GCJR方法：(1) 从执行依赖图中切片故障相关事件；(2) 构造图可行的单事件与成对候选集；(3) 通过配对的干净对应物回放验证候选集。

Result: 在120-DAG受控基准的90个范围内案例中，GCJR达到1.000家族精确匹配，平均回放调用次数从56.3降至25.3（减少55.1%）。在24例四智能体LLM试点中同样达到1.000家族精确匹配，平均模型调用从21.0降至10.0（减少52.4%）；并发现单事件回放会遗漏联合必要修复。

Conclusion: GCJR在固定回放结果的条件下，在其声明的图域内是精确的。该方法比穷举搜索大幅减少回放调用，并首次实现联合必要修复族的完整恢复，弥补了点状归因方法无法捕捉多事件协同故障的不足。

Abstract: Failures in agentic AI systems can arise from interactions among messages exchanged by multiple large language model (LLM) agents. Pointwise attribution cannot distinguish a jointly necessary repair from alternative singleton repairs. We formulate Minimal Repair Family Recovery (MRFR): recovering all inclusion-minimal event sets whose counterfactual replay restores task success within a declared size bound. We propose Graph-Constrained Joint Replay (GCJR), which slices failure-relevant events from an execution dependency graph, constructs graph-feasible singleton and pair candidates, and verifies them by replay with paired clean counterparts. For fixed replay outcomes, GCJR is exact within its declared graph domain. On 90 in-scope cases from a 120-DAG controlled benchmark, GCJR achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match while reducing mean replay calls from 56.3 to 25.3 (55.1%) relative to exhaustive search. On a 24-case, four-agent LLM pilot, it again achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match and reduces mean model calls from 21.0 to 10.0 (52.4%); single-event replay misses jointly necessary repairs.

</details>


### [270] [Validating FKG.in: Soundness Assessment in LLM-Augmented Indian Food Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29249)
*Saransh Kumar Gupta,Armaan Shah,Lipika Dey,Partha Pratim Das,Ramesh Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半自动化的食谱数据健全性评估工作流，用于验证由大语言模型（LLM）从非正式烹饪来源中提取和增强的结构化食谱数据。该流水线属于FKG.in印度食物知识图谱项目的一部分，通过形式语法、词汇检查、统计启发式、基于Set Transformer的一致性建模和检索验证等多阶段方法，识别并修复结构性不一致、语义逻辑不连贯以及偏离原文等常见失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线烹饪生态系统中，LLM生成、修改或摘要的食谱内容日益增多。尽管这些输出看似合理，但可能包含幻觉成分、错误数量或文化上不合理的组合，限制了其在下游应用和知识图谱构建中的可用性。因此需要一种系统化的方法来验证LLM增强食谱数据的准确性。

Method: 提出一个多阶段半自动评估流水线，包括：(1) 基于形式语法检查结构一致性；(2) 基于词汇表检查语义合理性；(3) 统计启发式来检测异常；(4) 基于Set Transformer的一致性建模；(5) 检索式验证以核对与源文本的一致性。该流水线整体处理结构性不一致、语义逻辑不连贯和源文本偏差三类主要失效模式。

Result: 在印度食谱上进行了评估，验证了该框架的有效性。虽然评估集中于印度菜系，但所提方法可推广至更广泛的多语言、多文化烹饪领域。

Conclusion: 提供了一个实用、可审计且与应用无关的框架，用于验证LLM增强的食谱数据，从而在LLM生成内容时代加强了机器可读食物知识基础设施的基础。

Abstract: The online culinary ecosystem is increasingly populated by recipe content generated, modified, or summarized by Large Language Models (LLMs).
